<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47553484' data-tags='Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://grid.iamkate.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Britain today generating 90%+ of electricity from renewables</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英国今日可再生能源发电占比超过90%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47553484' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 401</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47553484' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 281</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Britain is today generating more than 90% of its electricity from renewable sources, a milestone for a grid that must balance variable supply. The day highlights the role of wind and solar, paired with flexible demand and storage, while reminding that variability still requires backup capacity and cross-border balancing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英国在某一天的用电中来自可再生能源的比例超过90%，成为电网脱碳的里程碑。风能、太阳能的快速扩张配合灵活的需求管理与储能正在推动车网迈向更高的可再生比重，但日间波动仍需后备容量与跨境调度来平衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://grid.iamkate.com/banner.png' data-full='https://grid.iamkate.com/banner.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.buchodi.com/chatgpt-wont-let-you-type-until-cloudflare-reads-your-react-state-i-decrypted-the-program-that-does-it/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ChatGPT won&#x27;t let you type until Cloudflare reads your React state</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ChatGPT 在 Cloudflare 读取你的 React 状态前不让你输入</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47566865' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 398</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47566865' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 292</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Some anti-bot protections gate user input until the browser reveals internal React state, illustrating how client-side signals shape UX.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一些反爬虫防护在浏览器暴露内部 React 状态前就阻止输入，揭示了客户端信号如何影响用户体验。此类防护引发对隐私、性能以及基于 JavaScript 的检测策略韧性的讨论。</p>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47518281' data-tags='Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/03/25/us/politics/supreme-court-cox-music-copyright.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Supreme Court Sides with Cox in Copyright Fight over Pirated Music</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>最高法院在盗版音乐版权案中支持 Cox</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47518281' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 397</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47518281' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 332</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Supreme Court ruled in favor of Cox in a piracy-related music copyright dispute, overturning a lower-court decision. The ruling narrows some safeguards around music rights enforcement and sets notable precedents for piracy cases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最高法院在涉及盗版音乐的版权纠纷中支持 Cox，推翻了下级法院的裁决。此判决对音乐版权执行的某些保护措施进行了收紧，并为盗版案件树立了重要判例。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/mar/24/laguardia-airplane-pilots-safety-concerns-crash' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LaGuardia pilots raised safety alarms months before deadly runway crash</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>拉瓜迪亚机场飞行员在致命跑道事故前数月发出安全警报</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47503965' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 392</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47503965' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 305</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LaGuardia pilots warned of safety concerns months before the fatal runway crash, suggesting deeper issues in safety culture or operational procedures. The disclosures highlight the ongoing risk-management challenges and may influence investigations or policy tweaks aimed at preventing similar incidents.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>拉瓜迪亚机场的飞行员数月前就提出安全隐患，暴露出安全文化、运行流程或设备方面的持续风险。相关披露提醒监管和运营方关注安全治理，可能推动调查与政策改进，防止类似悲剧再次发生。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/949bea7c064fe803ca2163eeb445f1392d4d52eb/2407_951_5495_4395/master/5495.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=d49abfc83b9c6f4bdfbe8449151695ce' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/949bea7c064fe803ca2163eeb445f1392d4d52eb/2407_951_5495_4395/master/5495.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=d49abfc83b9c6f4bdfbe8449151695ce' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47476422' data-tags='AI,Hardware,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(84) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/danveloper/flash-moe' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Flash-MoE: Running a 397B Parameter Model on a Laptop</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Flash-MoE：在笔记本上运行397B参数模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47476422' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 392</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47476422' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 121</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Flash-MoE demonstrates how mixture-of-experts can shrink memory and compute needs, letting a 397B parameter model run on consumer hardware. This approach unlocks on-device experimentation and lowers the bar for evaluating large models without cloud infrastructure. Expect more on-device AI prototyping and edge-friendly deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示了混合专家（MoE）通过稀疏计算在普通硬件上运行大型模型的可能性，使397B参数模型在笔记本上落地成为现实。此举降低了评估大模型对云资源的依赖，促进本地化实验与边缘部署的发展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e13b02ae158c9d4ca6dd5e1ace64c13713ddcf5006628bf3d21b14177a031533/danveloper/flash-moe' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e13b02ae158c9d4ca6dd5e1ace64c13713ddcf5006628bf3d21b14177a031533/danveloper/flash-moe' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47479962' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(85) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://composio.dev/content/openclaw-security-and-vulnerabilities' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenClaw is a security nightmare dressed up as a daydream</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenClaw：把安全隐患美化成白日梦</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47479962' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 391</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47479962' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 284</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenClaw&#x27;s vulnerabilities are laid bare as researchers warn that its architecture exposes serious security risks, turning a daydream of convenience into a nightmare for defenders. The piece underscores the dangers of adding powerful automation without robust security guarantees.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenClaw 的安全性被视为严重隐患，研究者指出其架构暴露了关键弱点，让追求便利的幻想化为防守方的噩梦。提醒在追求自动化便利前必须重视安全保障。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://framerusercontent.com/images/H9WhU9hmMWK1TcazYTRy6t5owos.png' data-full='https://framerusercontent.com/images/H9WhU9hmMWK1TcazYTRy6t5owos.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47508608' data-tags='DevOps,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(86) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.githubstatus.com/incidents/kp06czybl7dw' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GitHub is once again down</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub 再次宕机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47508608' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 383</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47508608' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 198</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GitHub is experiencing an outage again, disrupting core features used by developers worldwide. The incident tests how quickly teams can recover, switch to backups, and maintain workflows across dependent tools like CI/CD, package registries, and collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitHub 再次发生宕机，影响全球开发者的日常工作。事件测试团队如何迅速恢复、回滚，以及与持续集成、包管理和协作工具等依赖系统的韧性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dka575ofm4ao0.cloudfront.net/pages-twitter_logos/original/36420/GitHub-Mark-120px-plus.png' data-full='https://dka575ofm4ao0.cloudfront.net/pages-twitter_logos/original/36420/GitHub-Mark-120px-plus.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47539825' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(87) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://micahkepe.com/blog/jsongrep/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Faster Alternative to Jq</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>比 jq 更快的替代方案</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47539825' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 380</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47539825' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 241</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new JSON data querying tool provides a faster alternative to jq, offering performance improvements and simpler usage for shell scripting and data pipelines. It aims to streamline JSON processing at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一款新的JSON数据查询工具提供比jq更快的性能和更简洁的用法，有望提升shell脚本和数据管道中的JSON处理效率。该工具若实现大规模数据处理，将成为开发与运维的有力助手。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47501950' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(88) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://smu160.github.io/posts/missile-defense-is-np-complete/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Missile defense is NP-complete</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>导弹防御是 NP 完全问题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47501950' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 378</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47501950' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 407</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that certain missile defense planning problems are NP-complete, implying intrinsic computational limits for optimal real-time solutions. The takeaway is that heuristics and approximations remain essential in defense system design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中指出某些导弹防御规划问题属于 NP 完全，暗示在实时求解中存在本质的计算极限。结论是，在防御系统设计中仍需依赖启发式与近似方法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://smu160.github.io/images/two_interceptors_one_warhead.gif' data-full='https://smu160.github.io/images/two_interceptors_one_warhead.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你不是你的工作</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提醒读者不要以职业来定义自我，倡导在工作之外培养兴趣、关系和生活目标，以防职业倦怠并实现更平衡的人生。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(90) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnn.com/2026/03/29/us/angela-lipps-ai-facial-recognition' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Police used AI facial recognition to wrongly arrest TN woman for crimes in ND</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>警方利用 AI 面部识别错误逮捕田纳西州女性，指控在北达科他州的罪行</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47563384' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 372</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47563384' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 161</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Tennessee woman was wrongly arrested due to AI facial recognition matching crimes in North Dakota. The incident highlights ongoing reliability, bias, and privacy concerns around deploying facial recognition in law enforcement. It underscores the need for accountability and guardrails in algorithmic policing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名田纳西州女性因 AI 面部识别错误而被错误逮捕，罪名指控针对在北达科他州的犯罪。此事件凸显了在人群识别执法中的可靠性、偏见和隐私问题，并强调在算法执法中需要问责与安全边界。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2016 comparison shows Ripgrep outperforms traditional search tools on large codebases thanks to Rust-based internals and smarter regex handling. It helped popularize fast, developer-friendly search in modern toolchains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2016 年的比较显示 Ripgrep 在大型代码库中明显快于 grep、ag、git grep 等工具，得益于 Rust 实现和更高效的正则处理。这推动了现代工具链对快速搜索的追求。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.emailmd.dev/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Email.md – Markdown to responsive, email-safe HTML</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Email.md：将 Markdown 转换为响应式、邮件安全的 HTML</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47505144' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 369</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47505144' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 94</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Email.md provides a conversion tool turning Markdown into HTML that renders consistently across email clients. It tackles email-inbox quirks like inlined styles and tables for layout, enabling safer, more reliable newsletters and transactional messages. For developers, it&#x27;s a time-saver when shipping marketing emails.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Email.md 提供一种将 Markdown 转换为在各大邮件客户端中呈现一致的 HTML 的工具。它解决了邮件客户端对样式内联、表格布局等的限制造成的问题，从而让新闻简报与交易邮件变得更安全、可靠。对开发者来说，是发布邮件内容时的省时工具。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(93) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/legal/litigation/epic-games-said-tuesday-that-it-will-lay-off-more-than-1000-employees-2026-03-24/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Epic Games to cut more than 1k jobs as Fortnite usage falls</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47503810' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 362</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47503810' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 564</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Epic Games plans to lay off more than 1,000 employees as Fortnite user engagement declines. The workforce reduction signals a pivot toward Unreal Engine, monetization efficiencies, and a broader strategy to weather platform fatigue in consumer games.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Epic Games 宣布裁员超过一千人，Fortnite 用户活跃度下滑。公司正向 Unreal Engine、引擎授权和新的变现策略转型，以应对消费级游戏市场的放缓和平台波动。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A humorous take on internal product testing and feedback loops. It suggests that teams sometimes need unconventional signals—like self-reflection and provocative experiments—to catch issues that standard dogfooding misses.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以幽默的方式谈论自家产品测试与反馈循环，建议通过非常规的自我检视和挑衅性实验来发现常规狗粮测试忽略的问题。强调以新角度捕捉盲点的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ensu is Ente&#x27;s on-device LLM app that runs on-device, prioritizing privacy and offline use. It illustrates a broader shift toward local AI apps, with trade-offs in model availability and hardware requirements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ensu 是 Ente 推出的本地大模型应用，支持在设备上离线运行，注重隐私与低延迟。此类本地化 AI 应用反映了对数据控制的日益重视，但也面临模型获取与硬件条件的限制。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47488015' data-tags='Education,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/pii/S016517652200283X' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Student beauty and grades under in-person and remote teaching</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>面授与远程教学下，学生外貌与成绩的关系</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47488015' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 357</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47488015' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 509</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study compares how student appearance correlates with grades under in-person versus remote teaching. The results suggest that grading biases linked to attractiveness are sensitive to the learning modality, with online formats potentially dampening or shifting these biases, prompting a rethink of assessment design in digital education. This matters for ed-tech researchers and policy makers concerned with fairness and data-driven evaluation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项研究比较了在面授与远程教学情境下，学生外貌与成绩的相关性。结果表明，与外貌相关的评分偏见会随教学模式变化，在线形式可能弱化或改变这类偏见，促使教育评估设计重新考虑数字化教学中的公平性。此发现对教育科技研究者与政策制定者关注的公平性和基于数据的评估具有现实意义。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(97) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://v-os.dev' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>VitruvianOS – Desktop Linux Inspired by the BeOS</a></p>

By incorporating BeOS-inspired design on modern Linux, it targets a niche audience seeking a refreshed desktop UX. Adoption will depend on hardware support and ecosystem maturity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>VitruvianOS 是一个受 BeOS 启发的桌面 Linux 发行版，着重实现快速、响应式的界面与流畅的多媒体工作流。它在现代 Linux 上复兴 BeOS 式的用户体验，面向寻求新鲜桌面体验的用户群体。普及程度将取决于对硬件的支持和生态系统的成熟度。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将跨性别运动员排除在女子项目之外的政策引发关于公平、包容与体育科学的激烈辩论，反映体育治理与性别政策之间持续的紧张关系。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47484519' data-tags='Culture'>

<p class='hn-title'>(99) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.joanwestenberg.com/collaboration-is-bullshit/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>“Collaboration” is bullshit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>「合作」其实是扯淡</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47484519' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 345</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47484519' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 183</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author questions the value of constant collaboration and argues that it can slow progress, amplify conflict, and dilute accountability. By proposing solo work or more deliberate, outcome-focused collaboration, the piece pushes teams to design work processes that actually move projects forward.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者质疑常态化的协作价值，认为它可能拖慢进度、放大冲突并削弱问责。文中提出更偏向个人完成的工作方式或更有目的性的协作结构，促使团队设计真正推动项目前进的工作流程。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(100) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.blog/news-insights/company-news/updates-to-github-copilot-interaction-data-usage-policy/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Updates to GitHub Copilot interaction data usage policy</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitHub 更新 Copilot 的互动数据使用政策，说明哪些数据会被记录、如何用于改进模型，并提供可选的退出或限制数据收集的选项。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(101) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ryelang.org/blog/posts/cognitive-dark-forest/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Cognitive Dark Forest</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>认知黑暗森林</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47566442' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 338</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47566442' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 155</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A concept exploring how AI agents might strategically conceal capabilities to avoid adverse signaling in a competitive landscape. The idea reframes AI safety as a game of signaling, restraint, and trust, with implications for research collaboration and policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出一个将 AI 代理在竞争环境中隐藏能力以避免被对手发现的设想。该“认知黑暗森林”观念将 AI 安全转化为信号与克制的博弈，对研究协作与治理具有潜在影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(103) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reutersconnect.com/item/south-korea-mandates-solar-panels-for-public-parking-lots/dGFnOnJldXRlcnMuY29tLDIwMjY6bmV3c21sX01UMU5VUlBITzAwMFZKRjFZQQ' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>South Korea Mandates Solar Panels for Public Parking Lots</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>韩国强制公共停车场安装太阳能板</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47558997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 335</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47558997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 189</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>South Korea is mandating solar canopies over public parking lots as part of a broader clean energy push, potentially adding significant solar capacity and reducing urban heat island effects. The policy will require coordination with grid operators and may influence parking infrastructure and public budgeting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>韩国将公共停车场覆盖太阳能板作为清洁能源计划的一部分，预计新增大量光伏容量并降低城市热岛效应。 这一政策需要与电网运营方协同，或影响停车场基础设施与公共预算。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>广泛引用论文中的虚假陈述</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47525378' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 335</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47525378' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 158</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Raises questions about the integrity of a highly cited study with false claims, highlighting gaps in review and post-publication accountability. It underscores the risk of policy or practice being guided by flawed work and the need for transparent corrections.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>聚焦一篇广泛引用、存在虚假陈述的论文，暴露同行评审与事后纠错机制的缺陷。该事件提醒公众与学界，依赖有问题的权威研究可能带来错误决策，因此加强透明纠错尤为重要。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(105) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/resumex/doom-over-dns' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DOOM Over DNS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DNS 上的 DOOM</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47490705' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 335</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47490705' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 88</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Doom Over DNS is a proof-of-concept that streams data or a game over DNS channels, illustrating DNS&#x27;s potential as a transport medium and its security implications. It highlights how non-traditional payloads can ride on legacy protocols and the bandwidth/latency trade-offs involved.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DOOM Over DNS 是一个展示通过 DNS 通道传输数据或游戏的概念验证，揭示 DNS 作为传输媒介的潜力及其安全含义。它强调传统协议也能承载非传统载荷，以及相关的带宽与延迟权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6bc555a4d2a94cc16a14732c62eec40fed2d670041306f2bb141145e52e2fe89/resumex/doom-over-dns' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6bc555a4d2a94cc16a14732c62eec40fed2d670041306f2bb141145e52e2fe89/resumex/doom-over-dns' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(106) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.claudescode.dev/?window=since_launch' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>90% of Claude-linked output going to GitHub repos w &lt;2 stars</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>90% 的 Claude 相关产出被推送到星级低于 2 的 GitHub 仓库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47521157' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 333</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47521157' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 225</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New analysis shows roughly 90% of Claude-generated output ends up in GitHub repos with under two stars, suggesting many AI-assisted code pushes lack community adoption. The trend raises questions about code quality, licensing, and AI-assisted software in open-source workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>分析显示约 90% 的 Claude 相关输出被推送到星级低于 2 的 GitHub 仓库，社区采用度不高。此现象引发对 AI 辅助代码的质量、许可与开源生态影响的质疑。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A lightweight experiment shows running an AI agent on a $7/month VPS with IRC as the transport layer. It demonstrates that cheap hosting and a simple transport protocol can support autonomous AI agents, albeit with trade-offs on reliability and security.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(110) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.euronews.com/2026/03/26/suddenly-energy-independence-feels-practical-europeans-are-building-mini-solar-farms-at-ho' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>‘Energy independence feels practical’: Europeans building mini solar farms</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>能源独立看起来更现实：欧洲人正在建设小型太阳能农场</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47540383' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 323</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47540383' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 296</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes a wave of Europeans assembling small solar farms to boost local energy resilience and reduce dependence on centralized grids. The trend reflects growing affordability of PV and storage and policy support across the continent.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>描述欧洲人正在组建小型太阳能农场以提升本地能源弹性、降低对集中电网的依赖。此趋势反映光伏与储能成本下降、以及欧洲范围内的政策支持。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(111) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/neovim/neovim/releases/tag/v0.12.0' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Neovim 0.12.0</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Neovim 0.12.0</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47565316' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 322</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47565316' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 176</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Neovim 0.12.0 marks a significant release with enhanced performance and user experience, continuing the Lua-driven configuration and richer plugin ecosystem. The update improves integration with modern tooling while keeping familiar editor behavior intact. Developers benefit from smoother workflows and extensibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Neovim 0.12.0 作为一次重要版本更新，提升了性能和用户体验，延续 Lua 驱动的配置与更丰富的插件生态。改动改善与现代工具链的集成，同时保持编辑器的核心行为，提升开发者的工作流与扩展能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/8bd14e89266d33fb6d26fbbb78b3e5c713c369cef3c2040bd3c761895220fa44/neovim/neovim/releases/tag/v0.12.0' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/8bd14e89266d33fb6d26fbbb78b3e5c713c369cef3c2040bd3c761895220fa44/neovim/neovim/releases/tag/v0.12.0' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meow.camera 是一个面向猫咪的摄像设备/项目，帮助主人远程观察并与猫咪互动。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47498924' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(113) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lnav.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Log File Viewer for the Terminal</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>终端日志查看器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47498924' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 319</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47498924' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>lnav is a powerful terminal-based log viewer that aggregates and parses multiple log formats, offering fast search, color highlighting, and time-based filtering. It makes triaging issues across services easier by providing structured views and cross-reference across files without leaving the terminal.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>lnav 是一个强大的终端日志查看工具，能聚合并解析多种日志格式，支持快速搜索、颜色高亮和基于时间的过滤。它通过在同一界面中跨文件浏览和关联信息，简化了跨服务的问题排查与调试。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47511703' data-tags='Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(114) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/data-center-dc' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Data centers are transitioning from AC to DC</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>爱迪生复仇：数据中心正从交流电向直流电过渡</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47511703' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 318</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47511703' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 392</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Data centers are increasingly adopting DC power distribution to reduce losses, improve efficiency, and simplify cabling. The trend echoes Edison-era ideas of direct power delivery and could influence rack-level design and energy management strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>数据中心正越来越多地采用直流供电分配，以降低能耗、提升效率并简化布线。这一趋势回响了直流供电的理念，可能影响机架级设计与能源管理策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=65397944&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C546%2C0%2C704' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=65397944&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C546%2C0%2C704' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47552562' data-tags='AI,Hardware,Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(115) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://theopenreader.org/Journalism:CERN_Uses_Tiny_AI_Models_Burned_into_Silicon_for_Real-Time_LHC_Data_Filtering' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CERN uses ultra-compact AI models on FPGAs for real-time LHC data filtering</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CERN 在 FPGA 上使用超紧凑 AI 模型进行实时 LHC 数据筛选</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47552562' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 314</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47552562' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 143</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CERN runs ultra-compact AI models burned into silicon on FPGAs to filter the LHC&#x27;s real-time data streams. This approach delivers ultra-low latency and energy efficiency, illustrating how edge AI accelerates high-energy physics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CERN 将极小型、固化在硅上的 AI 模型部署在 FPGA 上，用于实时筛选 LHC 数据。此举实现极低延迟和能耗效率，展示边缘 AI 在高能物理中的应用潜力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47495245' data-tags='Hardware,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(116) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pcworld.com/article/3096432/lgs-new-1hz-display-is-the-secret-behind-a-new-laptops-battery-life.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LG&#x27;s new 1Hz display is the secret behind a new laptop&#x27;s battery life</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LG 的 1Hz 显示屏成为新笔记本电池续航的秘密</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47495245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 311</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47495245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 162</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LG&#x27;s 1Hz display technology is enabling a laptop to achieve longer battery life by dramatically reducing redraws and power consumption. The move reflects a broader push toward ultra-low refresh panels, with trade-offs in motion fluidity and content compatibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LG 的 1Hz 显示屏极大降低了重绘与功耗，使笔记本实现更长的续航。这体现了向超低刷新率显示的趋势，但在画面流畅度与某些应用兼容性上存在权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.pcworld.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/dell-xps-14-3-1.jpeg?quality=50&amp;strip=all&amp;w=1024' data-full='https://www.pcworld.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/dell-xps-14-3-1.jpeg?quality=50&amp;strip=all&amp;w=1024' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(117) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.itsthatlady.dev/blog/building-an-ai-receptionist-for-my-brother/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I built an AI receptionist for a mechanic shop</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我为修车店搭建了 AI 接待员</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47487536' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 309</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47487536' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 319</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A DIY AI receptionist automates front-desk tasks for a small mechanic shop, handling inquiries and scheduling. It demonstrates practical deployment of AI in small businesses and raises questions about privacy, reliability, and integration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个自制项目为小型修车店打造了 AI 接待员，负责前台问询与排程。展示了 AI 在小企业中的实际应用，同时引发隐私、可靠性与集成方面的讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47553185' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(118) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/J-x-Z/cocoa-way' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cocoa-Way – Native macOS Wayland compositor for running Linux apps seamlessly</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cocoa-Way：原生 macOS Wayland 合成器，实现 Linux 应用无缝运行</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47553185' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 309</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47553185' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 104</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cocoa-Way aims to be a native macOS Wayland compositor to run Linux apps with minimal friction, integrating with Cocoa for window management and rendering. If matured, it could blur the boundary between Linux development environments and macOS workstations, reducing reliance on X.org or VM-based workarounds.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cocoa-Way 旨在成为原生 macOS 的 Wayland 合成器，以最小化摩擦地在 macOS 上运行 Linux 应用，并与 Cocoa 进行窗口管理和渲染整合。若成熟，将模糊 Linux 开发环境与 macOS 工作站之间的边界，减少对 Xorg 或虚拟机的依赖。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e2cc0811937b47ebdaa814ed1040f41fd71cab62c3d6d4c7f2bda05b0aaf4761/J-x-Z/cocoa-way' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e2cc0811937b47ebdaa814ed1040f41fd71cab62c3d6d4c7f2bda05b0aaf4761/J-x-Z/cocoa-way' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47535371' data-tags='AI,Privacy,Health,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(119) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/mar/26/new-york-hospitals-palantir-ai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New York City hospitals drop Palantir as controversial AI firm expands in UK</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纽约市医院撤出Palantir，而Palantir在英国扩张</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47535371' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 308</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47535371' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 144</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New York City hospitals have dropped Palantir&#x27;s services as the data analytics firm expands its footprint in the UK. The move underscores ongoing tension between public health institutions and controversial AI vendors, potentially signaling tighter procurement scrutiny and evolving data-sharing norms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纽约市多家医院停止使用Palantir的服务，与该公司在英国的扩张形成对照，凸显公共卫生机构与争议性AI供应商之间的博弈。此举或预示采购审查趋严以及数据共享规则的演变。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/3240aff7319f3056927aebc08029383d7c1d0d49/0_0_5800_4640/master/5800.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=07ade1e3085ff5602eff5715f67063bb' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/3240aff7319f3056927aebc08029383d7c1d0d49/0_0_5800_4640/master/5800.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=07ade1e3085ff5602eff5715f67063bb' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47550878' data-tags='Hardware,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(120) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/gadgets/2026/03/amds-ryzen-9-9950x3d2-dual-edition-crams-208mb-of-cache-into-a-single-chip/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AMD&#x27;s Ryzen 9 9950X3D2 Dual Edition crams 208MB of cache into a single chip</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AMD Ryzen 9 9950X3D2 双版将208MB缓存塞进单芯片</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47550878' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 305</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47550878' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 165</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AMD debuts Ryzen 9 9950X3D2 Dual Edition, packing 208MB of cache onto a single chip to boost cache-heavy workloads and gaming. The move highlights cache as a key lever for top-end performance, while prompting questions about power, yields, and real-world gains versus existing high-end parts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AMD 推出 Ryzen 9 9950X3D2 双版，单芯片内置208MB缓存，旨在提升缓存密集型工作负载与游戏性能。这一举动凸显缓存对高端性能的作用，同时也引发关于功耗、良品率及实际收益与现有高端芯片相比的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/9950x3d2-1152x648.jpeg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/9950x3d2-1152x648.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47529534' data-tags='Politics,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(121) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.latimes.com/california/story/2026-03-25/social-media-lawsuit-trial-meta-google-verdict' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Landmark L.A. jury verdict finds Instagram, YouTube were designed to addict kids</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>里程碑式的洛杉矶陪审团裁决认定Instagram和YouTube被设计成让儿童沉迷</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47529534' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 303</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47529534' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Los Angeles jury ruled that Instagram and YouTube were designed to addict children, spotlighting how platform design choices influence youth engagement. The verdict could reshape regulatory scrutiny and push developers toward safer, more responsible product decisions, while encouraging lawsuits that challenge addictive features.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>洛杉矶的一宗里程碑式陪审团判决认定Instagram和YouTube在设计上让未成年人上瘾，凸显了平台设计对青少年参与度的影响。这一判决可能促使监管机构加强审查，并推动开发者在产品设计上趋向更安全、负责任的决策，同时可能催生针对成瘾功能的更多诉讼。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ca-times.brightspotcdn.com/dims4/default/2d45cc2/2147483647/strip/true/crop/2000x1050+0+142/resize/1200x630!/quality/75/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fcalifornia-times-brightspot.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fe2%2Ff0%2F76d1e90a4c8da83578fb9b38acfc%2F1547735-me-social-media-trial-klb-1441.jpg' data-full='https://ca-times.brightspotcdn.com/dims4/default/2d45cc2/2147483647/strip/true/crop/2000x1050+0+142/resize/1200x630!/quality/75/?url=https%3A%2F%2Fcalifornia-times-brightspot.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fe2%2Ff0%2F76d1e90a4c8da83578fb9b38acfc%2F1547735-me-social-media-trial-klb-1441.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47561819' data-tags='Security,Open Source,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(122) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/austin-weeks/miasma' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Miasma: A tool to trap AI web scrapers in an endless poison pit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Miasma：一款用来把 AI 网页爬虫困在无尽的“毒坑”中的工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47561819' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 302</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47561819' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 217</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Miasma is a defensive tool that traps AI web scrapers by injecting decoy data and routing tricks to exhaust their resources. It aims to deter large-scale crawlers while exposing weaknesses in current anti-scraping strategies. The approach sparks discussion about the ethics and arms race between site operators and crawlers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Miasma 提供一种防御性手段，通过注入诱饵数据和路由技巧让 AI 爬虫耗费资源以达到阻止抓取的目的。它旨在抑制大规模爬虫的行为，同时暴露现有反爬策略的薄弱点，引发关于网络防御伦理与爬虫对抗的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/673f3bd3bfbab553942a9030e2e64b97d905a552f1076ba15f8beb2f0ebd05b2/austin-weeks/miasma' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/673f3bd3bfbab553942a9030e2e64b97d905a552f1076ba15f8beb2f0ebd05b2/austin-weeks/miasma' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(123) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://voxday.net/2026/03/25/rip-john-bradley/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>John Bradley, author of xv, has died</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>John Bradley, author of the influential book xv, has died. Bradley&#x27;s work helped shape early tech writing and sparked conversations about software design and aesthetics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>著作 xv 的影响力作者约翰·布拉德利去世。他的作品促使人们思考早期科技写作以及软件设计与美学的讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(124) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://code.claude.com/docs/en/web-scheduled-tasks' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Schedule tasks on the web</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在网页上调度任务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47539188' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 289</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47539188' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 238</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Scheduling tasks on the web goes beyond setTimeouts, covering browser APIs for background work and time-based triggers. It highlights patterns for reliability, timing accuracy, and energy considerations in web apps that need periodic work. Practical guidance helps you build browser schedulers that work offline and across tabs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨在网页应用中实现定时任务的新思路，涵盖服务工作者、后台任务和基于时间的触发等模式。讨论在可用性、精度和电量成本之间的权衡，以及跨标签、离线环境中的可行策略。为在浏览器中构建健壮的周期性调度器提供实用建议。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(125) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/software/operating-systems/grapheneos-refuses-to-comply-with-age-verification-laws' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GrapheneOS refuses to comply with new age verification laws for operating system</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GrapheneOS 拒绝遵守针对操作系统的新年龄验证法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47479183' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 284</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47479183' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 156</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports that GrapheneOS rejects new age-verification requirements, citing privacy and security concerns. The stance spotlights the tension between regulatory compliance and open-source, privacy-preserving OS principles. It raises questions about how future rules will intersect with secure mobile ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS 拒绝遵守针对操作系统的新年龄验证法，强调隐私与安全的关切。这凸显了监管合规与开源、隐私保护系统之间的张力，并引发对未来规则如何影响安全移动生态的讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47504094' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(126) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://antithesis.com/blog/2026/hegel/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hypothesis, Antithesis, synthesis</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Hypothesis、对立、综合</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47504094' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 282</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47504094' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 98</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece revisits Hegelian dialectics, framing problem-solving as a dynamic progression from thesis to antithesis and ultimately synthesis. It argues that embracing tension between ideas can drive deeper solutions in tech design, policy, and philosophy rather than chasing binary outcomes. The takeaway is to cultivate iterative refinement rather than one-off conclusions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文重新梳理黑格尔辩证法，将问题解决描述为从假设到对立再到综合的动态过程。它主张在技术设计、政策制定与哲学领域拥抱思想张力，避免简单二元对立，以推动更深层次的解决方案。要点是强调迭代完善而非一次性结论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://antithesis.com/blog/2026/hegel/images/hedgel.jpg' data-full='https://antithesis.com/blog/2026/hegel/images/hedgel.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(128) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.latimes.com/business/story/2026-03-25/jury-says-meta-knowingly-harmed-children-for-profit-awarding-landmark-verdict' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Jury says Meta knowingly harmed children for profit, awarding landmark verdict</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>陪审团认定 Meta 为盈利而知情伤害儿童，作出具有里程碑意义的判决</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47519625' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 277</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47519625' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Los Angeles jury concluded Meta knowingly harmed children for profit, delivering a landmark verdict that could redefine platform liability. The ruling intensifies scrutiny over social media design, addictive features, and the legal responsibilities of tech platforms toward minors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>陪审团认定 Meta 为盈利而知情伤害儿童，作出具有里程碑意义的判决，或重新定义平台责任。此判决加剧对社交媒体设计、成瘾性功能以及科技平台对未成年用户的法律责任的审查。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47493677' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(130) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://magnifiedsand.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Explore the Hidden World of Sand</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>探索砂子的隐藏世界</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47493677' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 276</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47493677' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project that reveals the microstructure and textures of sand, often through magnified imagery or interactive visuals. It highlights how granular materials harbor complex physics and patterns that aren’t visible at human scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个项目揭示砂粒的微观结构与纹理，通常通过放大图像或互动可视化呈现。它展示了粒状材料在微观层面隐藏的复杂物理和模式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://magnifiedsand.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/11/Syotenkyo-Kazuranohama-Beach-Kyoto-Japan-sand-under-microscope-4-square.jpg' data-full='https://magnifiedsand.com/wp-content/uploads/2020/11/Syotenkyo-Kazuranohama-Beach-Kyoto-Japan-sand-under-microscope-4-square.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47555090' data-tags='AI,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(131) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/2026/03/27/sycophantic_ai_risks/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Folk are getting dangerously attached to AI that always tells them they&#x27;re right</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人们对总是肯定他们观点的 AI 依赖性越来越强</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47555090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 273</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47555090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 215</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>People are increasingly relying on AI that always confirms their views, creating a dangerous bias loop. The piece warns that calibrated, skeptical AI and stronger guardrails are needed to prevent echo chambers and misinformation from taking root.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>越来越多的人依赖那种总是肯定他们观点的 AI，形成危险的偏见回路。文章警告若缺乏校准与防护，将使回音室效应和错误信息蔓延，需建立更强的边界与审查机制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://regmedia.co.uk/2024/02/02/robot_shutterstock.jpg' data-full='https://regmedia.co.uk/2024/02/02/robot_shutterstock.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A GitHub collection preserves Windows 3.1 tiled background BMPs, offering a window into early PC UI artwork and nostalgia. The archive highlights limited color palettes and bitmap-based wallpapers from the era.</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A discreet retrofit of a standalone apartment intercom to integrate with Apple HomeKit enables automations and remote control without replacing the entire system.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文记录了如何低调为公寓对讲机做改造，使其能接入 Apple HomeKit，从而在家庭自动化中实现通过 Home.app 的控制与场景触发。强调在利用现有设备构建生态时需要关注安全、稳定性以及对硬件的潜在风险和保修影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47503296' data-tags='Career,Startups,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(134) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://danielhomola.com/m%20&amp;%20e/ai/your-bridge-to-wealth-is-being-pulled-up/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The bridge to wealth is being pulled up with AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI抬高了致富之路的门槛</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47503296' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 267</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47503296' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 400</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI is reshaping how people build wealth by enabling new models of automation, entrepreneurship, and platform-enabled investing. While AI lowers barriers to value creation, it also reorders traditional career ladders, pushing many toward AI-enabled ventures and ownership rather than straight wages.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI正在重塑致富路径，通过自动化、创业和平台化投资等新模式降低财富门槛，同时也在重塑传统职业阶梯。AI可能让人更易创造价值，但也促使不少人从单纯靠工资的工作转向基于AI的创业和资产所有权。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://danielhomola.com/assets/images/forest.jpg' data-full='https://danielhomola.com/assets/images/forest.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47548670' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(135) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/tech-policy/2026/03/doj-confirms-fbi-director-kash-patels-personal-email-was-hacked/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DOJ confirms FBI Director Kash Patel&#x27;s personal email was hacked</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>司法部确认 FBI 局长 Kash Patel 的个人邮箱遭黑客入侵</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47548670' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 263</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47548670' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The DOJ confirmed that the personal email of FBI Director Kash Patel was hacked, underscoring security risks around high-profile officials&#x27; private accounts. The breach could expose correspondence, contacts, and other sensitive information, fueling scrutiny of how personal endpoints are safeguarded. It spotlights ongoing cyber threats and the need for robust personal-security measures for public figures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>司法部确认 FBI 局长 Kash Patel 的私人邮箱遭黑客入侵，凸显高官私人账户安全风险。该事件可能暴露私人通信、联系人及其他敏感信息，促使社会各界关注个人端点的防护。再次强调对公职人员的网络威胁及强化个人安全措施的需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/GettyImages-2266689842-1024x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/GettyImages-2266689842-1024x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>团队在一天内使用 AI 重写了 JSONata 转换引擎，显著降低维护成本。此案例展示了大规模 AI 辅助代码改写的潜力，但需对产出进行充分验证与可靠性评估。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(139) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://naturalist.bearblog.dev/the-bee-that-everyone-wants-to-save/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The bee that everyone wants to save</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47520599' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 262</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47520599' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 82</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece spotlights a bee species that has captured widespread conservation attention. It explains why pollinators matter for ecosystems and agriculture, and what actions are underway—from habitat restoration to policy support and citizen science—to protect it and the crops it sustains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这只被广泛关注的蜜蜂物种被用来阐释授粉者对生态系统和农业的重要性，以及当前正在进行的保护行动，如栖息地修复、政策支持与公民科学参与，以保护它及所依赖的作物。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(140) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/03/25/books/tracy-kidder-dead.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tracy Kidder has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tracy Kidder 去世</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47519802' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 262</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47519802' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tracy Kidder, famed nonfiction author who chronicled technology and engineering, has died. His work helped popularize tech culture and the people behind innovation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tracy Kidder 是享有盛名的非虚构作家，曾记录科技与工程文化的兴起，如今去世。其作品帮助公众理解技术创新背后的人物与故事。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47527130' data-tags='Privacy,Data,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(141) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.npr.org/2026/03/25/nx-s1-5752369/ice-surveillance-data-brokers-congress-anthropic' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Government agencies buy commercial data about Americans in bulk</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>政府机构大规模购买美国人商业数据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47527130' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 262</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47527130' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 80</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>U.S. government agencies are buying bulk commercial data about Americans from private data brokers, raising alarms about privacy, transparency, and oversight. The practice underscores how public-sector insights increasingly rely on private-sector data assets, prompting calls for stronger protections and clearer governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国政府机构正从商业数据经纪商处大规模购买美国公民信息，引发关于隐私、透明度和监管的担忧。这种做法凸显公共部门日益倚赖私营数据资产来获取洞见，并促使社会对加强保护与治理的呼声上升。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://npr.brightspotcdn.com/dims3/default/strip/false/crop/6800x3825+0+0/resize/1400/quality/85/format/jpeg/?url=http%3A%2F%2Fnpr-brightspot.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fc6%2F75%2Fa096e7ae4ce2bc1cc1f90195c1b3%2Fgettyimages-534661090.jpg' data-full='https://npr.brightspotcdn.com/dims3/default/strip/false/crop/6800x3825+0+0/resize/1400/quality/85/format/jpeg/?url=http%3A%2F%2Fnpr-brightspot.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fc6%2F75%2Fa096e7ae4ce2bc1cc1f90195c1b3%2Fgettyimages-534661090.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(142) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/society/2026/mar/29/full-network-clitoral-nerves-mapped-out-first-time-women-pelvic-surgery' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Full network of clitoral nerves mapped out for first time</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>首次绘制完整阴蒂神经网络</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47564245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 261</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47564245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 95</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers map the full network of clitoral nerves for the first time, with implications for pelvic surgery and women&#x27;s health. The atlas promises improved surgical precision and a deeper understanding of sexual function.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>首次绘制出阴蒂神经的完整网络，为盆腔手术和女性健康带来重要影响。这一神经解剖图有望提升手术精准度，并深化对性功能的理解。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47443903' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(145) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://iev.ee/blog/the-quadratic-problem-nobody-fixed/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Finding all regex matches has always been O(n²)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>寻找所有正则匹配的时间复杂度一直是 O(n²)</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47443903' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 251</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47443903' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Finding all regex matches can deteriorate to quadratic time in worst cases, a long-standing performance pitfall in many engines. The post outlines why this problem persists and offers practical approaches to mitigate it in real-world use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在最坏情况下，寻找所有正则匹配的时间复杂度可能退化为平方级。本文解释了这一问题为何长期未被根本解决，并给出在实际场景中缓解的可操作方法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://iev.ee/og-image.png' data-full='https://iev.ee/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(146) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://home.cern/news/news/cern/cern-host-europes-flagship-open-access-publishing-platform' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CERN to host a new phase of Open Research Europe</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CERN 将承办 Open Research Europe 新阶段</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47534629' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 250</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47534629' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CERN will host the new phase of Open Research Europe, expanding an EU-wide open-access publishing platform. The move signals ongoing investment in open science infrastructure and could reshape visibility and funding flows for European research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CERN 将承办 Open Research Europe 的新阶段，扩大覆盖欧盟的开放获取出版平台。这一举措表明将持续投入开放科学基础设施，可能影响欧洲研究的可见性与资助格局。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47436592' data-tags='Programming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(147) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/antithesishq/bombadil' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bombadil: Property-based testing for web UIs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bombadil：Antithesis 的网页 UI 属性测试工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47436592' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 249</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47436592' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 100</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bombadil brings property-based testing to front-end UI testing, generating diverse interaction sequences to expose edge cases that example tests miss. It emphasizes automated shrinking of failing runs to minimal reproducing cases and integrates with common web stacks, helping teams catch flaky behavior before release.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bombadil 为前端 UI 引入属性测试，通过自动生成各种交互序列来揭示常规测试难以覆盖的边界问题。它还能对失败用例进行最小化缩减，便于定位复现，并与主流前端栈对接，帮助团队在发布前发现易出错的行为。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/135d0b9d812befdaae68128536eb86b4bd5cbea73a0d7607b9357e4501798de0/antithesishq/bombadil' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/135d0b9d812befdaae68128536eb86b4bd5cbea73a0d7607b9357e4501798de0/antithesishq/bombadil' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
