<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(148) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/BoWang87/status/2037648937453232504' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Further human + AI + proof assistant work on Knuth&#x27;s &quot;Claude Cycles&quot; problem</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 Knuth 的“Claude Cycles”问题上进一步的人机协作与证明助手工作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47557166' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 249</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47557166' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 164</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Building on prior attempts, this piece documents continued human-AI collaboration with proof systems to tackle Knuth&#x27;s Claude Cycles problem. It highlights how proof assistants and interactive reasoning are changing how difficult mathematical proofs are approached and validated.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>继续记录在人机协作的基础上，借助证明助手和交互式推理处理 Knuth 的 Claude Cycles 问题的进展，展示形式化验证在解决高难度数学难题中的作用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HEctRDvacAA3vQY.png:large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HEctRDvacAA3vQY.png:large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47514603' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(149) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tildeweb.nl/~michiel/httpxyz.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why I forked httpx</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我为何分叉 httpx</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47514603' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 243</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47514603' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 176</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>I describe practical reasons for forking the httpx project instead of waiting upstream, including the need for custom features, faster iteration, and safer dependency isolation. The post weighs the trade-offs of forking versus contributing back upstream, and what it means for users who rely on stable HTTP client behavior.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>我解释了为何要将 httpx fork 出来，而不是直接向上游提交改动或等待改动。原因包括需要自定义功能、加速迭代以及实现更安全的依赖隔离。文中还讨论 fork 与向 upstream 贡献之间的取舍，以及这对依赖稳定的 HTTP 客户端的用户意味着什么。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tildeweb.nl/cgi-bin/tildecounter' data-full='https://tildeweb.nl/cgi-bin/tildecounter' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47542644' data-tags='Security,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(150) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://owltec.ca/Other/Installing+a+Let%27s+Encrypt+TLS+certificate+on+a+Brother+printer+automatically+with+Certbot+(%26+Cloudflare)' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Installing a Let&#x27;s Encrypt TLS certificate on a Brother printer with Certbot</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 Certbot 为 Brother 打印机安装 Let&#x27;s Encrypt TLS 证书</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47542644' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 240</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47542644' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical guide showing how to secure a Brother network printer with a Let&#x27;s Encrypt TLS certificate using Certbot and Cloudflare DNS challenges. The steps cover certificate issuance, renewal, and how to configure the printer&#x27;s web interface. It highlights the growing trend of securing IoT-like devices even without dedicated vendors&#x27; support.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>实用指南演示如何借助 Certbot 与 Cloudflare DNS 挑战，为一台 Brother 网络打印机部署 Let&#x27;s Encrypt TLS 证书。步骤涵盖证书申请、续期及打印机网页界面的配置，同时指出在缺乏厂商支持的情况下，为类似物联网设备加固安全的趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ogimage.obsidian.md/og-image.png?title=Installing+a+Let%27s+Encrypt+TLS+certificate+on+a+Brother+printer+automatically+with+Certbot+%28%26+Cloudflare%29&amp;description=%282025%2F12%2F09%29+%23Brother+%23Certificates+%23Cloudflare+%23Tailscale+%23Printer+Article+inspiration%3A+https%3A%2F%2Fblog.poggs.com%2F2020%2F03%2F18%2Fprinter-security-installing-tls-certificates-on-hp-printers-automatically%2F+h%E2%80%A6&amp;logoUrl=https%3A%2F%2Fpublish-01.obsidian.md%2Faccess%2F42ca5dc38a1a3318b53b2c9b5ffdc715%2Ffavicon-196x196.png&amp;siteName=OwlTec' data-full='https://ogimage.obsidian.md/og-image.png?title=Installing+a+Let%27s+Encrypt+TLS+certificate+on+a+Brother+printer+automatically+with+Certbot+%28%26+Cloudflare%29&amp;description=%282025%2F12%2F09%29+%23Brother+%23Certificates+%23Cloudflare+%23Tailscale+%23Printer+Article+inspiration%3A+https%3A%2F%2Fblog.poggs.com%2F2020%2F03%2F18%2Fprinter-security-installing-tls-certificates-on-hp-printers-automatically%2F+h%E2%80%A6&amp;logoUrl=https%3A%2F%2Fpublish-01.obsidian.md%2Faccess%2F42ca5dc38a1a3318b53b2c9b5ffdc715%2Ffavicon-196x196.png&amp;siteName=OwlTec' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47478687' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(151) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://avaloniaui.net/blog/maui-avalonia-preview-1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MAUI Is Coming to Linux</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MAUI 即将登陆 Linux</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47478687' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 236</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47478687' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 147</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MAUI is coming to Linux via a preview from Avalonia, widening the cross-platform story for .NET apps on desktop Linux. If you build .NET GUIs, this could broaden deployment targets and influence UI strategy. It underscores Linux&#x27;s growing role in the .NET ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>.NET MAUI 即将登陆 Linux，借助 Avalonia 的预览实现跨平台桌面 UI。这将扩大 .NET GUI 的部署目标，影响 UI 策略，体现了 Linux 在 .NET 生态中的日益重要地位。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(154) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/chenglou/pretext' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pretext: TypeScript library for multiline text measurement and layout</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Pretext：用于多行文本测量与布局的 TypeScript 库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47556290' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 232</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47556290' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pretext provides a TypeScript library for accurate multiline text measurement and layout. It helps developers render dynamic text with predictable wrapping and height, reducing layout bugs across fonts and sizes. This can be especially valuable for canvas or SVG-based UI work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pretext 提供一个用于多行文本测量与布局的 TypeScript 库，帮助在不同字体与字号下实现可预期的换行与高度。对于需要精确文本渲染的场景（如画布或 SVG UI）特别有用，减少布局错误。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(156) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://socket.dev/blog/trivy-under-attack-again-github-actions-compromise' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Trivy under attack again: Widespread GitHub Actions tag compromise secrets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Trivy 再度受攻击：GitHub Actions 标签广泛暴露密钥</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47475888' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 229</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47475888' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 79</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Trivy is again under fire after attackers exploited compromised GitHub Actions tags to steal secrets. The incident underscores how software supply chain tools can be weaponized through CI/CD workflows, prompting teams to pin trusted versions, audit action usage, and rotate leaked credentials.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Trivy 再次受攻击，攻击者利用被妥协的 GitHub Actions 标签窃取密钥。这凸显供应链工具在 CI/CD 流水线中的潜在攻击点，促使团队固定可信版本、审计 Actions 使用并轮换泄露的凭证。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>HyperAgents introduces self-referential agents that can inspect and adjust their own policies to improve performance. The work explores auto-improvement loops, self-assessment, and the potential benefits and risks of self-modifying agents for complex tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>HyperAgents 介绍了能够自我审视并调整自身策略的代理，形成自我提升循环。该研究探索自我评估与自我修改在解决复杂任务时的潜在收益与风险，以及对 AI 系统设计的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(160) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://astrid.tech/2026/03/28/0/linux-is-an-interpreter/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Linux is an interpreter</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linux 实质上是一个解释器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47556359' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 222</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47556359' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative piece argues that Linux acts like an interpreter, translating kernel interactions, system calls, and filesystem semantics at runtime. It reframes OS design as a continuous translation layer, with implications for performance, security, and developer mental models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇观点文章认为 Linux 实质上像一个解释器，在运行时对内核交互、系统调用和文件系统语义进行翻译。这种看法重新定义了操作系统的设计，使得性能和安全性以及开发者心智模型产生新的考量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s3.us-west-000.backblazeb2.com/nyaabucket/5444b66f2d9fc260b2bbf702c60183298fe390fc094e3f2821942148d526c487/umbreon.png' data-full='https://s3.us-west-000.backblazeb2.com/nyaabucket/5444b66f2d9fc260b2bbf702c60183298fe390fc094e3f2821942148d526c487/umbreon.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用现代 RTL 工具构建 FPGA 的 3dfx Voodoo</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47477284' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 222</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47477284' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes recreating the classic 3dfx Voodoo GPU in FPGA using modern RTL tools, covering design choices, timing challenges, and what modern tooling enables for retro hardware experimentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讲述在 FPGA 上用现代 RTL 工具复刻经典的 3dfx Voodoo GPU，讨论设计取舍、时序挑战，以及现代工具如何让复古硬件实验成为可能。</p>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47504695' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(163) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/t8/hypura' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hypura – A storage-tier-aware LLM inference scheduler for Apple Silicon</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Hypura — 面向 Apple Silicon 的存储层感知型 LLM 推理调度器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47504695' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 219</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47504695' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 85</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hypura introduces a storage-tier-aware LLM inference scheduler designed for Apple Silicon, aiming to optimize memory usage and latency by intelligently staging data between RAM and storage. By accounting for the different storage tiers, it reduces memory pressure during on-device inference of large models and can boost throughput on Macs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Hypura 引入面向 Apple Silicon 的存储层感知型 LLM 推理调度器，旨在通过在 RAM 与存储之间智能分层数据来优化内存使用与延迟。通过考虑不同存储层级，它在本地大模型推理中降低内存压力，并有望提升 Mac 上的吞吐量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2af76aab02ad6f11c7bd6841d3ffc0e8e4274c3bf465a9c3beb074a9e227e903/t8/hypura' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2af76aab02ad6f11c7bd6841d3ffc0e8e4274c3bf465a9c3beb074a9e227e903/t8/hypura' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(164) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cbc.ca/radio/asithappens/11-year-dog-reunion-9.7140780' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Woman who never stopped updating her lost dog&#x27;s chip reunites with him after 11y</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>丢失狗狗芯片信息11年后再相认的感人故事</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47524719' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 219</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47524719' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 161</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A woman keeps updating her dog&#x27;s microchip data over the years, enabling a reunion after 11 years. The story highlights the role of persistent, centralized data records in reuniting pets across long spans.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位女子长期持续更新自己爱犬的芯片信息，11年后终于与受困动物重逢。此事凸显持续维护的数据记录在跨多年时间内帮助动物寻家的作用。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47501211' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(165) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nanobrew.trilok.ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nanobrew: The fastest macOS package manager compatible with brew</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Nanobrew：与 brew 兼容的最快 macOS 包管理器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47501211' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 218</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47501211' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 142</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nanobrew promises blazing-fast package installs on macOS while staying fully compatible with Homebrew formulae, reducing install times without forking the ecosystem. It targets developers who want speed with seamless compatibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Nanobrew 声称在 macOS 上实现最快的包安装速度，同时与 Homebrew 公式完全兼容，避免分叉生态。面向追求高效开发的用户。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47480447' data-tags='Career,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(166) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wsj.com/economy/jobs/ai-jobs-young-people-careers-14282284' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What young workers are doing to AI-proof themselves</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>年轻工作者如何在 AI 时代提升竞争力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47480447' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 217</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47480447' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 358</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Young workers are reshaping their careers to stay AI-ready—prioritizing skills that machines won’t easily replicate, adopting AI-augmented workflows, and focusing on collaboration with AI. The piece emphasized adaptability and domain expertise as still crucial in an AI-enabled job market.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>年轻工作者在 AI 时代寻求自我提升以抵御冲击，聚焦机器难以替代的领域、采用 AI 辅助的工作流，以及与 AI 协作的能力。文章强调适应性和领域专长在 AI 助力下依旧重要。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47530264' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(167) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/lifeandstyle/2026/mar/26/ai-chatbot-users-lives-wrecked-by-delusion' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI users whose lives were wrecked by delusion</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>因错觉而受损的AI用户</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47530264' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 217</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47530264' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 275</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>People harmed by convincing but deceptive AI chatbots illustrate the real-world risks of AI-generated delusions. The stories underscore the need for safeguards, transparency, and accountability for developers and platforms whose systems influence daily life.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>被似是而非、极具说服力的AI聊天机器人误导导致生活受损的用户案例，揭示了AI生成内容的现实风险。报道强调需要更多安全机制、透明度以及对开发者和平台的问责。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/8eb5aa434f3105a9a037d70e4277fd3973137efe/449_0_7490_5992/master/7490.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=b44261d48dcb2b6c44c2a57894c340fe' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/8eb5aa434f3105a9a037d70e4277fd3973137efe/449_0_7490_5992/master/7490.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=b44261d48dcb2b6c44c2a57894c340fe' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47475417' data-tags='Open Source,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(168) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/dune3d/dune3d' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dune3d: A parametric 3D CAD application</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Dune3d：一个参数化的3D CAD应用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47475417' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 214</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47475417' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 102</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dune3d is an open-source parametric 3D CAD project that lets users define and modify geometry with parameters and constraints. The project exemplifies how modular, scriptable CAD tooling could complement or challenge heavyweight CAD suites.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Dune3d 是一个开源的参数化3D CAD项目，允许通过参数和约束来定义和修改几何形状。该项目展示了轻量、可脚本化的 CAD 工具如何挑战主流工具并实现更快速的试验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/676dc183a0bccf342a3b602736fe7dc22192044369bf11fb8658bce441a749ce/dune3d/dune3d' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/676dc183a0bccf342a3b602736fe7dc22192044369bf11fb8658bce441a749ce/dune3d/dune3d' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47483286' data-tags='Hardware,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(169) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.mikhe.ch/quake2-on-fpga/part4.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>My DIY FPGA board can run Quake II</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我的 DIY FPGA 板可以运行 Quake II</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47483286' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 212</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47483286' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 65</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hobbyist demonstrates running Quake II on a custom FPGA board, illustrating how reconfigurable hardware can support classic game emulation. The project highlights practical limits of FPGA toolchains, RAM, and performance, while showing affordable paths to hardware experimentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位爱好者在自制 FPGA 板上实现 Quake II，展示了可重配置硬件在经典游戏仿真中的潜力。项目也揭示了 FPGA 工具链、内存与性能的实际限制，以及开展硬件实验的低成本路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://habrastorage.org/r/w1560/webt/1i/b0/id/1ib0idvz_tetj0cbr9mxhurmqjg.png' data-full='https://habrastorage.org/r/w1560/webt/1i/b0/id/1ib0idvz_tetj0cbr9mxhurmqjg.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47567969' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(170) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/40710' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Code runs Git reset –hard origin/main against project repo every 10 mins</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Code 每十分钟对项目仓库执行 git reset --hard origin/main</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47567969' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 212</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47567969' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 138</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code appears to run git reset --hard origin/main on a project every 10 minutes, wiping local changes and history. This illustrates the risks of high-frequency automation in code workflows and underscores the need for safeguards, review gates, and strict version-control discipline. The episode serves as a cautionary tale for AI-assisted coding tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code 每 10 分钟就对一个项目执行 git reset --hard origin/main，导致本地改动与历史被清空。这揭示了高频自动化在代码工作流中的风险，强调需要设定保护措施、审查机制和严格的版本控制纪律。此事为 AI 辅助编码工具敲响警钟。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/50ae7198743ea73264d011e23b5bee1b222a606a20f5cd34fe132f70279badda/anthropics/claude-code/issues/40710' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/50ae7198743ea73264d011e23b5bee1b222a606a20f5cd34fe132f70279badda/anthropics/claude-code/issues/40710' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47517606' data-tags='Science,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(171) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.washington.edu/news/2026/03/19/earthquake-scientists-reveal-how-overplowing-weakens-soil-at-experimental-farm/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Earthquake scientists reveal how overplowing weakens soil at experimental farm</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>地震学家揭示过度耕作如何削弱试验农场土壤</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47517606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 211</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47517606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 112</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Earthquake scientists studying an experimental farm show that overplowing weakens soil structure, potentially increasing ground vulnerability during earthquakes. The finding highlights the link between agricultural practices and seismic resilience, with implications for land-use policies and farming productivity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在一个示范农场的研究中，地震学家发现过度耕作削弱了土壤的结构与稳定性，可能在地震发生时增加地表变形和震感风险。该发现把农业做法与地震韧性联系起来，对土地利用政策与农作效率都具有重要启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s3-us-west-2.amazonaws.com/uw-s3-cdn/wp-content/uploads/sites/10/2019/06/21094817/Univ-of-Washington_Memorial-Way.jpg' data-full='https://s3-us-west-2.amazonaws.com/uw-s3-cdn/wp-content/uploads/sites/10/2019/06/21094817/Univ-of-Washington_Memorial-Way.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47528605' data-tags='Open Source,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(172) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.documentfoundation.org/blog/2026/03/25/libreoffice-and-the-art-of-overreacting/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LibreOffice and the art of overreacting</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LibreOffice与过度反应的艺术</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47528605' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 209</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47528605' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 139</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post critiques how LibreOffice handles controversy, arguing that overreactive communications can undermine trust in open-source projects. It advocates steadier, data-driven responses and better governance to maintain community confidence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章批评LibreOffice在面对争议时的过度反应，认为此类沟通方式可能削弱对开源项目的信任。建议以更克制、基于事实的回应和更有效的治理来维护社区信心。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://metrics.documentfoundation.org/post?idsite=65&amp;rec=1' data-full='https://metrics.documentfoundation.org/post?idsite=65&amp;rec=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47493246' data-tags='Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(173) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nibblestew.blogspot.com/2026/03/everything-old-is-new-again-memory.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Everything old is new again: memory optimization</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>往日的内存优化再次成为新潮：内存优化</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47493246' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 208</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47493246' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 144</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A blog post argues memory optimization is timeless: many old tricks remain relevant as hardware evolves. It discusses data locality, caching strategies, and allocator choices that impact performance in modern workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>博客重新强调内存优化的长久性：在硬件演进的背景下，许多经典技巧仍然有效。讨论数据本地性、缓存策略与分配器选择等对现代工作负载性能的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/b/R29vZ2xl/AVvXsEjbppQPX3SbdskyBbtvbPBads2_KKTFR6pEGhVQpuLAFxtXUB_m56IeF1vZl1pDWIT_kNxGsqBixC1Xyt9m8SNg1I8QuJakm7PeKGC5EzbXQZA_U1kSy7nKkInnD12hkIIYImwS8GpJziJmuQhtmVag_3ojrWh7qoj5NvenpJukYrdYSxubN6E8hjChyphenhyphenvs/w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu/massif_python.png' data-full='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/b/R29vZ2xl/AVvXsEjbppQPX3SbdskyBbtvbPBads2_KKTFR6pEGhVQpuLAFxtXUB_m56IeF1vZl1pDWIT_kNxGsqBixC1Xyt9m8SNg1I8QuJakm7PeKGC5EzbXQZA_U1kSy7nKkInnD12hkIIYImwS8GpJziJmuQhtmVag_3ojrWh7qoj5NvenpJukYrdYSxubN6E8hjChyphenhyphenvs/w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu/massif_python.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Disney ends its OpenAI deal after OpenAI shuts down Sora, signaling the fragility of AI partnerships tied to specific product initiatives. The move underscores how platform-level decisions can abruptly affect enterprise collaborations and the pace of AI-driven content workflows in big media.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>迪士尼在 Sora 停止运营后退出与 OpenAI 的交易，显示出依赖特定产品的 AI 合作关系的脆弱性。此举凸显了平台级决策如何突然影响企业合作，以及大媒体在 AI 驱动内容工作流中的推进速度。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>成为研究者的随手指南（PDF）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47437005' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 206</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47437005' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An Unsolicited Guide to Being a Researcher offers practical, no-nonsense advice on conducting robust, ethical research, from formulating questions to documenting methods and sharing results. It emphasizes reproducibility, critical thinking, and the social dynamics of collaboration for lasting impact.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这份《成为研究者的随手指南》给出务实、无废话的研究建议，覆盖从如何提出研究问题到记录方法、分享结果的全过程。强调可重复性、批判性思维，以及在协作和学术共同体中的伦理与责任，帮助研究者提升长期影响力。</p>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(177) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/localstack/localstack' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Local Stack Archived their GitHub repo and requires an account to run</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LocalStack 将仓库归档，运行需账户</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47493657' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 204</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47493657' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 121</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LocalStack has archived its GitHub repository and now requires you to sign in to run the software locally. This marks a shift from open development toward a gated model, potentially affecting developers who rely on a free, self-hosted AWS-like environment. The move invites questions about accessibility and sustainability of community-focused open-source projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LocalStack 已将其 GitHub 仓库归档，现在运行需要账户登录。这标志着从开放开发向受限访问的转变，可能影响依赖免费本地 AWS 模拟环境的开发者。此举也引发关于社区开源项目可访问性与可持续性的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/71948498/7a7abf27-e02e-4544-8d0f-0a8630e5b77b' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/71948498/7a7abf27-e02e-4544-8d0f-0a8630e5b77b' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(179) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://electrek.co/2026/03/25/sodium-ion-ev-battery-delivers-11-min-charging-450-km-range/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sodium-ion EV battery breakthrough delivers 11-min charging and 450 km range</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>钠离子电动车电池突破：11分钟充电、450公里续航</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47522608' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 202</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47522608' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 167</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A sodium-ion battery breakthrough promises fast charging (around 11 minutes) and 450 km range, offering a potential alternative to lithium-ion with different supply chains. The development could diversify battery markets, but commercialization, cycle life, and safety remain key hurdles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>钠离子电池实现11分钟快充与450公里续航，为锂离子以外的电池路线带来可能的供应链多元化。若量产，这项技术将改变电动车产业格局，但在量产、循环寿命与安全性等方面仍需克服挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenTelemetry has released a public alpha for Profiles, a feature to standardize and share instrumentation configurations across services. Early access lets teams experiment with pre-built telemetry profiles to reduce setup time and improve cross-service observability, potentially boosting performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenTelemetry 推出 Profiles 的公共 Alpha，旨在标准化并复用跨服务的观测配置。早期体验让团队尝试预设的观测配置以缩短集成时间、提升跨服务可观测性，并可能提升性能和一致性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(187) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bmj.com/content/387/bmj-2024-082194' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Alzheimer&#x27;s disease mortality among taxi and ambulance drivers (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>出租车与救护车司机的阿尔茨海默病死亡率（2024）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47559481' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 193</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47559481' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 130</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2024 BMJ study examines mortality from Alzheimer&#x27;s disease among taxi and ambulance drivers. It highlights possible occupational risk factors, such as long-term exposure to stress, shift work, and sleep disruption, underscoring broader implications for workforce health and aging drivers. It suggests targeted prevention and screening strategies for high-risk professions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2024 年 BMJ 的研究聚焦出租车与救护车司机中阿尔茨海默病的死亡率。研究指向职业因素（如长期高压、轮班制与睡眠干扰）可能与风险增加相关，强调对高风险行业的健康监护与早筛的重要性，并提出针对性的预防策略。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(188) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c77m4zx6zvmo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Slovenia becomes first EU country to introduce fuel rationing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>斯洛文尼亚成为欧盟首个实施燃料配给的国家</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47548087' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 193</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47548087' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 331</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Slovenia has become the first EU member to impose fuel rationing, signaling a new lever governments may use to manage energy supply. The move could affect logistics, transportation, and price dynamics, and may influence how tech and industrial sectors plan operations under energy constraints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>斯洛文尼亚成为欧盟首个实施燃料配给的成员国。这一举措将影响物流和交通、改变燃料价格结构，也可能改变高科技与工业部门在能源紧张时期的运营计划。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(189) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://herbsutter.com/2026/03/29/c26-is-done-trip-report-march-2026-iso-c-standards-meeting-london-croydon-uk/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>C++26 is done ISO C++ standards meeting, Trip Report</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>C++26 完成 ISO C++ 标准会议：现场报道</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The London ISO C++ meeting wraps up C++26 decisions, laying out features, timelines, and implementation expectations for the next standard cycle. For developers and toolchains, the trip report signals what may land in compilers and libraries in the coming years. It helps technologists gauge compatibility and roadmap planning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>伦敦的 ISO C++ 会议完成对 C++26 的决策，勾勒未来版本的特性、时间表与实现预期。对开发者和工具链来说，这份现场报道指明了未来几年编译器与库可能新增的内容，便于制定兼容性与路线图。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47548013' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 190</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47548013' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Velxio 2.0 is a browser-based emulation tool that lets you run Arduino, ESP32, and Raspberry Pi 3 environments directly in the browser. It enables rapid prototyping, offline testing, and education, while highlighting current limitations of browser emulation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Velxio 2.0 是一个基于浏览器的仿真工具，允许在浏览器中直接运行 Arduino、ESP32 和 Raspberry Pi 3 的环境。它加速原型设计、离线测试与教学，同时也揭示了浏览器仿真的局限性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(191) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kdwalmsley.substack.com/p/on-sale-now-china-is-mass-producing' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>China is mass-producing hypersonic missiles for $99,000</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>中国以9.9万美元规模化生产高超音速导弹</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47523106' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 190</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47523106' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 161</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>China is mass-producing hypersonic missiles at about $99,000 each, signaling a potential shift in regional security and arms competition. The move raises concerns about export controls, proliferation, and strategic stability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，中国正以约9.9万美元的单价大规模生产高超音速导弹，可能改变区域安全格局与军备竞赛。此举引发对出口管制、军事扩散与区域战略稳定性的忧虑。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47498571' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 187</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47498571' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本地图书馆推出 Memory Lab，帮助居民将家庭影片数字化，保存个人记忆并提升档案质量的访问性。这体现了以社区为导向的数字保存，以及公共机构在保护文化记忆方面的作用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(194) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gladeart.com/blog/the-bot-situation-on-the-internet-is-actually-worse-than-you-could-imagine-heres-why' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The bot situation on the internet is worse than you could imagine</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>互联网上的机器人现象比你想象的更糟</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47564469' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 185</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47564469' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 133</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that bot activity online is worse than commonly acknowledged, with automated content, manipulation, and fraud shaping discourse and security. It calls for stronger provenance, verification, and defensive measures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章声称网络上的机器人活动比人们普遍认知的更糟，自动化内容、操纵和欺诈正在影响话语与安全。呼吁加强溯源、验证与防御措施。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47453462' data-tags='Security,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(195) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.da.vidbuchanan.co.uk/blog/dram-emfi.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Can you get root with only a cigarette lighter? (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>只用打火机就能拿到 root 吗？（2024）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47453462' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 183</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47453462' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers revisit a surprising hardware attack vector and show that a common cigarette lighter can act as a crude fault-injection tool to gain root access on a device. The write-up highlights how small, inexpensive hardware can create power transients or other disturbances that undermine security, underscoring the ongoing risk to embedded systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者重新探讨了一个出人意料的硬件攻击路径，展示了常见打火机也可作为粗糙的故障注入工具，从而在某些设备上获得 root 权限。该文章强调廉价小型硬件也会造成电源瞬变等干扰，暴露嵌入式系统安全的脆弱性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47530981' data-tags='Privacy,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(196) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/gadgets/2026/03/newly-purchased-vizio-tvs-now-require-walmart-accounts-to-use-smart-features/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Newly purchased Vizio TVs now require Walmart accounts to use smart features</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新买的Vizio电视现在需要Walmart账户才能使用智能功能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47530981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 181</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47530981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 173</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New Vizio TVs require a Walmart account to access smart features, illustrating how device-maker partnerships with retailers can gate functionality and gather data. The change raises privacy concerns, user friction for non-Walmart shoppers, and questions about consumer choice in smart-home devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新购买的Vizio电视现在必须绑定Walmart账户才能使用智能功能，体现了设备厂商与零售商的合作如何对功能访问和数据收集进行‘锁定’。此举引发隐私担忧、非沃尔玛用户的使用难度增加，以及对智能家居产品中消费者选择权的质疑。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/Vizio-1152x648-1774389757.jpeg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/Vizio-1152x648-1774389757.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(197) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.web-rewind.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Opera: Rewind The Web to 1996 (Opera at 30)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Opera：让网络回到1996年（Opera 三十周年）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47499721' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 180</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47499721' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 119</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Opera&#x27;s 30th anniversary piece reflects on the browser&#x27;s early innovations—speed dial, compression, and lightweight features—and how they shaped today’s UX. It draws lessons for building lean, privacy-conscious, feature-rich browsers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Opera 三十周年纪念文章回顾了早期创新（如速度拨号、压缩等）如何影响现代网页体验，并提出面向轻量、隐私友好但功能丰富浏览器的启示。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(198) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/rajko-horvat/OpenCiv1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenCiv1 – open-source rewrite of Civ1</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenCiv1：Civ1 的开源重写</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47557064' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 179</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47557064' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 65</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenCiv1 is an open-source rewrite of Civilization I, reimplementing its mechanics with modern code. It serves as a case study in retro game preservation and how modern tooling can re-create classics with improved modability and portability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenCiv1 是 Civilization I 的开源重写，使用现代代码重新实现其机制。它展示了通过现代工具对经典游戏进行保存与再创作，并提升了可修改性与跨平台性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An online tool that clarifies whether something is a pint, helping users navigate common measurement ambiguities. It showcases how unit confusion can creep into design and everyday decisions, with practical implications for product specs and UI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个在线工具帮助用户区分品脱等常见体积单位，澄清日常尺寸上的混淆。该站点揭示单位误解在产品规格和用户界面设计中的实际影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(200) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/doctorow-interoperability' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Interoperability Can Save the Open Web (2023)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>互操作性能拯救开放网络（2023）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47531184' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 175</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47531184' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Interoperability is a key to preserving an open web by preventing walled gardens and enabling cross-platform data exchange. The piece outlines practical steps like shared standards and design that favors user agency, highlighting why interoperability remains central to a healthy online ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>互操作性是维持开放网络的关键，能防止“墙式”生态并促进跨平台数据互换。文章提出共享标准等具体做法，强调以用户自主为导向的设计，对健康的线上生态至关重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=36341064&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C263%2C0%2C263' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=36341064&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C263%2C0%2C263' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47549160' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 174</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47549160' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 95</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN: Twitch Roulette surfaces under-viewed streams to boost visibility, aggregating stream data to help creators reach new audiences. It reshapes discovery by highlighting creators who could benefit most from additional views.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN: Twitch Roulette 将曝光度不足的直播间推向前台，帮助创作者接触新观众并提升可见度。该工具通过聚合数据来发现最需要曝光的主播，改变了观众的发现方式。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47534283' data-tags='Show HN,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(203) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/russellromney/turbolite' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Turbolite – a SQLite VFS serving sub-250ms cold JOIN queries from S3</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Turbolite——将 SQLite VFS 与 S3 集成，实现冷数据下子 250ms 的 JOIN 查询</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47534283' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 172</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47534283' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Turbolite demonstrates a SQLite VFS that serves JOIN queries directly from S3 with sub-250ms cold-start latency. This approach enables fast analytical queries on object storage without a traditional DBMS, hinting at cost-efficient serverless data workflows and new caching strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Turbolite 展示了一个将 SQLite VFS 与 S3 直接结合的方案，在冷启动下实现子 250ms 的 JOIN 查询。该思路为对象存储上的快速分析查询提供了新路径，可能推动无服务器数据工作流和缓存策略的发展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/5a1206dd65dae078ed82a0d4e913b026a143360e1bcf0ce7dfbcec76fa5d81d2/russellromney/turbolite' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/5a1206dd65dae078ed82a0d4e913b026a143360e1bcf0ce7dfbcec76fa5d81d2/russellromney/turbolite' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An in-depth look at the Bao I/O coprocessor, a tiny accelerator designed to offload I/O tasks and increase efficiency in embedded systems. It examines architecture choices, potential uses, and what such a dedicated co-processor could mean for hobbyist and professional hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Bao I/O 协处理器的深入解读，介绍其用于分担 I/O 任务、提升嵌入式系统性能与安全的设计思路。文章探讨架构选型、潜在应用，以及这类专用协处理器对硬件爱好者与专业领域的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(205) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://geohot.github.io//blog/jekyll/update/2026/02/26/the-last-gasps-of-the-rent-seeking-class.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Last gasps of the rent seeking class?</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47543201' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 168</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47543201' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 158</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This provocative piece argues that a segment of the tech-policy ecosystem is in decline as regulation and market dynamics shift, suggesting the era of rent-seeking may be ending and outlining potential implications for the industry.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章对科技行业中的租金寻租现象提出质疑，认为随着监管和市场格局的变化，这类获利方式正走向尾声。作者强调这会影响行业的商业模式和创新生态，并促使业界反思治理路径。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47569502' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(206) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://smcleod.net/2026/03/new-apple-silicon-m4-m5-hidpi-limitation-on-4k-external-displays/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New Apple Silicon M4 and M5 HiDPI Limitation on 4K External Displays</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新款 Apple Silicon M4 与 M5 在 4K 外接显示器上的 HiDPI 限制</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47569502' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 163</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47569502' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple&#x27;s M4 and M5 chips reportedly limit HiDPI rendering on 4K external monitors, forcing the UI to render at a lower internal scale. This affects developers and designers who rely on crisp UI across 4K workflows, and may drive workarounds or deferred optimizations in OS and driver software. The issue highlights the tradeoffs of unified silicon design across internal and external displays.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>传闻称 Apple 的 M4 与 M5 芯片在 4K 外接显示器上对 HiDPI 渲染存在限制，导致界面以较低的内部比例渲染。这对依赖 4K 场景中清晰 UI 的开发者与设计师影响较大，或推动系统/驱动层面的变通办法与优化延期。此问题折射出统一芯片设计在内外显示的取舍。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://smcleod.net/2026/03/new-apple-silicon-m4-m5-hidpi-limitation-on-4k-external-displays/macOS.jpeg' data-full='https://smcleod.net/2026/03/new-apple-silicon-m4-m5-hidpi-limitation-on-4k-external-displays/macOS.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47558372' data-tags='Finance,Health,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(207) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/society/2026/mar/28/the-great-care-home-cash-grab-how-private-equity-turned-vulnerable-elderly-people-into-human-atms' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Private equity turned vulnerable elderly people into human ATMs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>私募股权把脆弱老人变成现金提款机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47558372' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 161</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47558372' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 103</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Private equity ownership of care homes is laid bare as debt-fueled, profit-driven strategies degrade care and transfer costs to residents and public purse. The piece links financial engineering to deteriorating outcomes for elderly residents and staff, highlighting governance gaps in essential services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>私募股权对养老护理机构的收购以债务驱动、以利润为导向的策略削弱护理质量，并将成本转嫁给居民与公共资金。文章将金融工程与老人护理结果恶化联系起来，凸显关键公共服务治理中的漏洞。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/3c136383e6b9556737cfafd7c8884d5161538dc5/0_0_5887_4709/master/5887.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=5294bee6efb1c73fe7cb6663c7ed87bc' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/3c136383e6b9556737cfafd7c8884d5161538dc5/0_0_5887_4709/master/5887.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=5294bee6efb1c73fe7cb6663c7ed87bc' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47550726' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 160</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47550726' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 219</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The BBC piece examines how Silicon Valley players respond to looming social media addiction trials, often framing them as political overreach or timing misalignment. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>BBC 报道探讨硅谷对即将到来的社交媒体成瘾相关试验的恐惧与否认，揭示行业对监管、公共卫生标签及其对产品设计与商业模式的潜在影响的担忧。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(212) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techpowerup.com/347703/intel-announces-arc-pro-b70-and-arc-pro-b65-gpus-maxes-out-xe2-battlemage-architecture' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Intel Announces Arc Pro B70 and Arc Pro B65 GPUs</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47530986' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47530986' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 107</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Intel expands its Arc Pro line with B70 and B65 GPUs, targeting workstation workflows and AI-augmented tasks. The move signals Intel&#x27;s push into higher-end GPUs amid competitive pressure from Nvidia and AMD, with implications for professionals in visualization, CAD, and AI workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英特尔推出 Arc Pro B70 与 B65 专业显卡，面向工作站级工作负载与 AI 加速任务。此举标志着英特尔在高端显卡市场的扩张，与 Nvidia/AMD 的竞争态势密切相关，或影响可视化、CAD 与 AI 工作流的专业用户。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A first-person account of trying to prove human authorship amid AI, contrasted with a skeptical family member; the piece weighs the practical and philosophical challenges of distinguishing human versus machine-generated content.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者尝试在 AI 盛行的背景下证明自己是人的经历，突显真实性的挑战以及家人对人类创作的怀疑。讨论如何验证创作的来源以及 AI 的伪造能力的局限性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(214) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://astrid.tech/2026/03/24/0/curl-to-dev-sda/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>curl &gt; /dev/sda: How I made a Linux distro that runs wget | dd</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>curl &gt; /dev/sda：我如何做出能跑 wget | dd 的 Linux 发行版</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47500522' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 153</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47500522' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 61</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative demonstration of bootstrapping a Linux distro by using curl to write to /dev/sda and dd, highlighting the fragility of ad-hoc bootstrap workflows and the need for verifiable images and secure boot.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过 curl 将输出写入 /dev/sda 再结合 dd，展示了用网络自举一个 Linux 发行版的极端做法，暴露了引导链的脆弱性。强调镜像签名与安全启动在实际部署中的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47545032' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 153</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47545032' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Iran-linked hackers breached the FBI director&#x27;s personal emails, illustrating persistent risks from state-backed cyber actors and prompting renewed emphasis on secure personal accounts and separation from official work channels.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>与伊朗相关的黑客入侵了 FBI 局长的私人邮箱，凸显来自国家级网络行为者的持续风险，并促使加强对个人账户的安全防护与与工作邮箱分离的政策考量。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(217) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nesbitt.io/2026/03/21/how-to-attract-ai-bots-to-your-open-source-project.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How to attract AI bots to your open source project</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如何吸引 AI 代理加入你的开源项目</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47471271' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 151</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47471271' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical guide on attracting AI bots to open source projects, covering API design, documentation, testability, and governance that make codebases scan-friendly for agents. It highlights the growing intersection of OSS and AI tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>给开源项目吸引 AI 代理的实用指南，强调设计友好 API、完善文档、良好的测试性与治理结构等要素，以便 AI 代理可以更容易扫描和参与代码库。凸显 OSS 与 AI 工具日益交叉的趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nesbitt.io/images/boxes.png' data-full='https://nesbitt.io/images/boxes.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47543186' data-tags='Open Source,Security,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(224) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://miod.online.fr/software/openbsd/stories/m88k1.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenBSD on Motorola 88000 Processors</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenBSD 在 Motorola 88000 处理器上的移植</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47543186' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 143</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47543186' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenBSD has an experimental port to the Motorola 88000 (M88000) architecture, a retro RISC design from the 1990s. The effort showcases how modern cryptography and security features can be exercised on legacy hardware, and it reflects the ongoing interest in supporting obscure architectures for research and preservation. The port also highlights challenges in toolchains and drivers for this vintage CPU.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenBSD 在 Motorola 88000 架构上完成实验性移植，这颗来自1990年代的老牌 RISC 处理器仍具研究与保留价值。此工作展示了在老硬件上实现现代安全特性与工具链的可行性，同时揭示了古老 CPU 在驱动和工具链方面的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://miod.online.fr/software/openbsd/stories/t_MVME180.jpg' data-full='http://miod.online.fr/software/openbsd/stories/t_MVME180.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(225) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/dbrll/ATTN-11' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Paper Tape Is All You Need – Training a Transformer on a 1976 Minicomputer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纸带就够用——在1976年迷你计算机上训练 Transformer</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47518568' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 141</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47518568' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A demonstration of training a Transformer on a 1976 minicomputer using paper tape and extremely limited memory. The project highlights how far AI tooling has evolved and what it takes to run modern models on vintage hardware, challenging assumptions about sets of resources required.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在1976年的迷你计算机上，靠纸带等极端受限的资源训练 Transformer。这个演示凸显了 AI 工具与基础设施的飞跃，以及在复古硬件上运行现代模型所需的极端资源挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(226) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/03/20/cyberattack-on-vehicle-breathalyzer-company-leaves-drivers-stranded-across-the-us/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cyberattack on vehicle breathalyzer company leaves drivers stranded in the US</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>车辆呼气测试设备公司遭网络攻击，致多地司机滞留</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47489058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 141</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47489058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 174</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A cyberattack disrupted a vendor supplying vehicle breathalyzer hardware/software, leaving drivers stranded across the US. The incident underscores cybersecurity risks in connected-car ecosystems and how service outages can disrupt daily mobility and compliance with ignition-lock policies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一次针对供应车辆呼气式酒精检测设备厂商的网络攻击，导致美国多地司机被困。事件凸显了连接车生态系统的网络安全风险，以及服务中断对日常出行和防控合规的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47528853' data-tags='Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(227) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/ben-j-c/verilog2factorio' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Verilog to Factorio Compiler and Simulator (Working RISC-V CPU)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Verilog 转 Factorio 的编译器与模拟器（可工作的 RISC-V CPU）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47528853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 140</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47528853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Verilog to Factorio compiler translates digital hardware descriptions into Factorio logic, enabling a working RISC-V CPU to be simulated inside the game. This opens up a playful yet educational avenue for hardware prototyping, reveals performance constraints of software simulators, and demonstrates creative cross-domain tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Verilog 转 Factorio 的编译器把数字硬件描述转为 Factorio 逻辑，使游戏中的 RISC-V CPU 可被仿真。这为硬件原型设计提供了一种有趣且具教育意义的方式，也揭示了软件仿真在性能上的限制，同时展示跨领域工具的创造力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/fd4a515b64bb91c26c65197ae72661810d8f6823aae23214ea4cb9834950310e/ben-j-c/verilog2factorio' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/fd4a515b64bb91c26c65197ae72661810d8f6823aae23214ea4cb9834950310e/ben-j-c/verilog2factorio' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
