<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(228) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://manualdousuario.net/en/how-to-kindle-personal-newspaper/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I turned my Kindle into my own personal newspaper</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我把 Kindle 打造成我的个人新闻报纸</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47541969' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 140</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47541969' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reader describes turning a Kindle into a personalized newspaper by piping in RSS feeds and curated sections, delivering a distraction-free, bespoke flow of news. The setup demonstrates how consumer devices can be repurposed for lightweight information workflows with minimal tooling. It offers practical tactics for staying informed in a focused way.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者将 Kindle 改造成个性化新闻报纸，通过订阅 RSS 源和自定义栏目，打造无干扰、量身定制的新闻流。该做法展示了如何用常见设备和简单工具实现高效的信息工作流。为在信息泛滥环境中保持专注提供了实用方法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://manualdousuario.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/leituras-web-kindle.jpg' data-full='https://manualdousuario.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/leituras-web-kindle.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47523169' data-tags='Health,AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(229) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.rnz.co.nz/news/national/590645/health-nz-staff-told-to-stop-using-chatgpt-to-write-clinical-notes' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Health NZ staff told to stop using ChatGPT to write clinical notes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新西兰卫生部提醒员工停止使用 ChatGPT 撰写临床笔记</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47523169' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 138</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47523169' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Health NZ staff are instructed to stop using ChatGPT for writing clinical notes, citing concerns about accuracy and patient privacy. The move reflects growing caution around AI-assisted medical documentation and governance needs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新西兰卫生部要求员工停止使用 ChatGPT 编写临床笔记，原因是准确性与患者隐私方面的担忧。此举反映出对 AI 医学文档的治理需求日益迫切。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.rnztools.nz/rnz/image/upload/s--c5SNbs78--/t_tohu-badge-facebook/v1762823835/4JY3TR9_nsq_episode_image_healthcare_v2_png?_a=BACCd2AD' data-full='https://media.rnztools.nz/rnz/image/upload/s--c5SNbs78--/t_tohu-badge-facebook/v1762823835/4JY3TR9_nsq_episode_image_healthcare_v2_png?_a=BACCd2AD' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(230) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://colibri.social/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Colibri – chat platform built on the AT Protocol for communities big and small</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Colibri — 基于 AT Protocol 的社区聊天平台，面向大小社区</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47533283' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 138</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47533283' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 98</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Colibri builds a chat platform on the AT Protocol to support federated communities of all sizes, highlighting the growing ecosystem of interoperable, decentralized social apps and the potential for scalable, privacy-respecting conversations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Colibri 构建了一个基于 AT Protocol 的聊天平台，面向大小不同的社区实现去中心化的跨实例对话。该项目凸显了可互操作的分布式社交应用生态，以及在隐私保护与跨机构协作中的潜力。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47542722' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(231) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.gadgetreview.com/hong-kong-police-can-now-demand-phone-passwords-under-new-security-rules' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hong Kong police can now demand phone passwords under new security rules</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>香港警方在新安全规则下可强制索取手机密码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47542722' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 137</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47542722' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 159</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New security rules expand police powers to request phone passwords, often with minimal safeguard requirements. Civil-liberties advocates warn this could erode privacy protections and set a precedent for broader surveillance. The change will influence how individuals defend their devices and how tech platforms design access controls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新安全规则扩大了警方对手机密码的索取权，通常几乎不设太多防护措施。公民自由倡导者警告这可能侵蚀隐私保护并为更广泛的监控定下先例。此举将影响个人如何捍卫设备、安全工具与平台的访问控制设计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.gadgetreview.com/wp-content/uploads/Depositphotos_169835510_XL-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://www.gadgetreview.com/wp-content/uploads/Depositphotos_169835510_XL-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47557901' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(232) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.androidauthority.com/android-sideload-carry-over-3652845/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Android’s new sideload settings will carry over to new devices</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Android 新的侧载设置将随设备迁移</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47557901' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 136</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47557901' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 200</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Android is rolling over sideload allowances across devices, so users enabling unknown sources won&#x27;t have to re-enable on every new device. The convenience comes with higher security risk, potentially broadening attack surfaces if devices are compromised or when switching accounts. It also signals a shift toward service- or account-level preferences rather than device-level toggles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Android 将把侧载设置跨设备携带，用户在换设备时不再需要重新开启未知来源。此举提高了使用便利性，但也提升了安全风险，若设备被攻破或账户切换时可能增加攻击面。体现了将偏好与设置从单一设备迁移到账户/服务级别的趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.androidauthority.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/04/Install-from-Unknown-Sources-2.jpg' data-full='https://www.androidauthority.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/04/Install-from-Unknown-Sources-2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47499672' data-tags='Show HN,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(233) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/AmElmo/proofshot' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: ProofShot – Give AI coding agents eyes to verify the UI they build</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: ProofShot – 让 AI 编码代理具备验证 UI 的能力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47499672' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 135</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47499672' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 91</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ProofShot enables AI coding agents to inspect and verify the UI they generate, acting as a visual verifier for automated UI construction. This reduces misalignment between intended design and rendered output, accelerating trusted AI-driven UI workflows. It showcases a practical approach to injecting human-in-the-loop feedback into automated UI pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ProofShot 让 AI 编码代理能够检查并验证它们生成的 UI，作为对自动化 UI 构建的可视化校验工具。它减少了设计意图与最终呈现之间的错配，加速了 AI 驱动的 UI 工作流程中的信任建立。展示了将人类在环反馈引入自动化 UI 流程的实际方法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6e2c49ee39ec54fd53114429b0b0d349e5dc35c31aaa7fa15cffd6539c5e3c93/AmElmo/proofshot' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6e2c49ee39ec54fd53114429b0b0d349e5dc35c31aaa7fa15cffd6539c5e3c93/AmElmo/proofshot' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(234) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/future/article/20260325-an-unstoppable-mushroom-is-tearing-through-north-american-forests' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An unstoppable mushroom is tearing through North American forests</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一种不可阻挡的蘑菇正在横扫北美森林</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47536102' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 135</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47536102' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 79</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A remarkable outbreak of a mushroom species is spreading through North American forests, threatening ecosystems and native trees. Researchers are racing to understand its spread mechanisms and mitigation options, as climate change and altered forest dynamics enable rapid expansion.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一种不可阻挡的蘑菇正在北美森林扩散，威胁生态系统和本地树种。科学家正加紧研究其扩散机制与治理策略，气候变化和森林生态的变化让其蔓延速度加快。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0n8q080.jpg' data-full='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0n8q080.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47553403' data-tags='Finance,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(235) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://paulkrugman.substack.com/p/treason-in-the-futures-markets' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Treason in the Futures Markets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>期货市场中的背叛行为</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47553403' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 134</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47553403' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 60</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Paul Krugman argues that incentives and structural flaws in futures markets threaten price discovery and market integrity. He points to regulatory gaps and opaque practices that reward short-term bets over transparent risk pricing, calling for reforms to restore trust and stability. The piece blends economics with policy implications for investors and regulators.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>保罗·克鲁格曼指出，期货市场的激励结构与制度缺陷正在损害价格发现与市场诚信。某些不透明的做法让短期投机优先于透明的风险定价，暴露监管空白，他呼吁改革以重建信任与稳定。该文将经济学观点与监管对策结合起来，为投资者与监管机构提供洞见。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(236) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lab.puga.com.br/cog/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: A plain-text cognitive architecture for Claude Code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：用于 Claude Code 的纯文本认知架构</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47524704' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 134</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47524704' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Presents a lightweight, plain-text cognitive architecture designed to orchestrate Claude Code&#x27;s reasoning and coding tasks. The approach favors human-readable prompts and modular planning over heavy tooling, lowering the barrier to experimenting with AI-assisted coding.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍一个轻量级的纯文本认知架构，用于编排 Claude Code 的推理和编码任务。该方法强调可读的提示与模块化计划，而非依赖复杂工具，降低 AI 辅助编码的试验门槛。</p>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾 486 处理器时代之后的发展，梳理 CPU 与 PC 架构的演化及背后的推动力量。将历史里程碑与当前设计取向联系起来。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(238) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dynomight.net/coffee/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LLMs predict my coffee</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大型语言模型预测我的咖啡</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47425874' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 132</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47425874' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A playful experiment where an LLM tries to predict your coffee order from context, highlighting both AI&#x27;s approachable utility and its limits in day-to-day decisions. The exercise illustrates how language models can augment personal assistant tasks, but privacy and accuracy concerns remain.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个有趣的实验：大型语言模型试图根据上下文预测你的咖啡订单，展示了 AI 在日常助理任务中的潜力与局限。该练习突显了语言模型在生活场景中的应用价值，同时也引发关于隐私和准确性的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(239) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.gjlondon.com/blog/ai-agents-could-make-free-software-matter-again/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Coding Agents Could Make Free Software Matter Again</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>编码代理可能让自由软件再次有意义</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47568028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 132</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47568028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 122</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI coding agents could lower barriers to contributions in free software, automating routine tasks and enabling more people to participate. The piece argues agents might revitalize community-driven software by accelerating collaboration and maintenance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>人工智能编码代理有望降低贡献自由软件的门槛，自动化常规任务，让更多人参与其中。文章认为代理能通过加速协作与维护来重振以社区为驱动的软件生态。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47489620' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(240) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ratfactor.com/openbsd/pf-gateway-bedtime' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>My home network observes bedtime with OpenBSD and pf</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A home network project uses OpenBSD and pf to enforce a bedtime schedule, blocking or throttling traffic after designated hours. It demonstrates practical, privacy-conscious network management at home.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个家庭网络项目通过 OpenBSD 与 pf 实现就寝时间表，在指定时间段阻断或限制网络流量。展示了在家中进行实用且注重隐私的网络管理方法。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>费城法院下周起全面禁止智能眼镜</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47569471' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 131</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47569471' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Philadelphia courts will ban all smart glasses starting next week due to privacy and security concerns in courtrooms. The move underscores how public institutions grapple with wearables in sensitive environments and could influence policy in other jurisdictions. Tech-enabled courtroom processes may need redesign or stricter controls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>费城法院将于下周起全面禁止智能眼镜，原因在于法庭场所的隐私与安保考量。这一举措凸显公共机构在敏感场景中对可穿戴设备的挑战，可能影响其他地区的相关政策。使用科技的法庭流程或需重新设计并加强管控。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>庆祝 IBM 科学家 Charles Bennett 因信息物理的贡献荣获图灵奖，强调他在信息理论方面的奠基性贡献。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(244) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonsafar.com/2025/running_dns/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>You can run a DNS server (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>到 2025 年你也可以运行 DNS 服务器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47453738' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 127</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47453738' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 88</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical look at running your own DNS server in 2025, covering recursive vs authoritative roles, privacy-first options like DoH/DoT, and deployment tips. Self-hosted DNS remains valuable for control, privacy, and reliability as public resolvers evolve.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2025 年自建 DNS 服务器的实用指南，涵盖递归与权威 DNS 的区分、隐私优先的 DoH/DoT 方案，以及部署要点。自建 DNS 仍然在可控性、隐私和可靠性方面具备价值，尤其在公共解析服务不断演进之时。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47547602' data-tags='Politics,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(245) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://coloradonewsline.com/briefs/surveillance-pricing-wage-setting/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Colorado House passes bill to limit surveillance pricing and wage setting</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科罗拉多州众议院通过法案，限制基于监控数据的定价与工资设定</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47547602' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 126</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47547602' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Colorado&#x27;s bill targets how data gathered through surveillance affects pricing and pay decisions. It would restrict dynamic pricing practices and the use of real-time data for setting pay, with aims of reducing discrimination and increasing transparency. If enacted, it could push companies to redesign data pipelines towards fairness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>科罗拉多州众议院通过法案，限制利用监控数据进行定价与工资决策的做法。法案拟约束基于数据的动态定价以及用实时数据设定薪酬的行为，旨在减少歧视并提高透明度。若成为法律，可能促使企业重构数据流程，以提升公正性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(247) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://riseproject.dev/2026/03/24/announcing-the-rise-risc-v-runners-free-native-risc-v-ci-on-github/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The RISE RISC-V Runners: free, native RISC-V CI on GitHub</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RISE RISC-V 运行者：在 GitHub 上的免费原生 RISC-V CI</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47531576' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 126</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47531576' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RISE announces free, native RISC-V CI runners on GitHub, lowering barriers to building and testing software for RISC-V hardware. This empowers open-source projects to validate builds without relying on x86 infrastructure, accelerating ecosystem innovation. It signals broader industry support for RISC-V viability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RISE 在 GitHub 上推出免费、原生的 RISC-V CI 运行器，降低了为 RISC-V 硬件构建和测试的门槛。开源项目可以不依赖 x86 基础设施即可验证构建，推动生态创新，体现业界对 RISC-V 可行性的广泛支持。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Questions the traditional QA role and suggests rethinking how quality assurance fits in modern, fast-moving development with automation and integrated testing.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(249) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://atgreen.github.io/repl-yell/posts/whistler/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Whistler: Live eBPF Programming from the Common Lisp REPL</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Whistler：在 Common Lisp REPL 中进行实时的 eBPF 编程</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47495190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 125</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47495190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47454127' data-tags='Programming,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(250) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://neon.com/blog/ctrl-c-in-psql-gives-me-the-heebie-jeebies' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The way CTRL-C in Postgres CLI cancels queries is incredibly hack-y</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PostgreSQL CLI 的 Ctrl-C 取消查询实现极其 hacky</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47454127' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 124</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47454127' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Postgres&#x27; psql uses an unusually hacky method to cancel running queries via Ctrl-C, which can leave transactions in limbo and occasionally trigger inconsistent client-server state.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>psql 在按 Ctrl-C 取消正在执行的查询时采用一种相当 hacky 的实现，可能让事务进入悬而未决的状态，甚至引发客户端与服务器之间的不一致。文章强调取消机制的脆弱性，以及为开发者和运维人员带来更清晰、无竞争条件的协议的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://neondatabase.wpenginepowered.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/neon-ctrl-c.jpg' data-full='https://neondatabase.wpenginepowered.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/neon-ctrl-c.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47540388' data-tags='Security,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(251) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://telnyx.com/resources/telnyx-python-sdk-supply-chain-security-notice-march-2026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Telnyx package compromised on PyPI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Telnyx Python SDK 的 PyPI 包遭遇供应链安全事件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47540388' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 123</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47540388' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 127</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Telnyx warns that one of its Python packages on PyPI was compromised, prompting a security advisory and remediation steps. The incident underscores the ongoing risk of supply-chain attacks in the Python ecosystem and the need for provenance checks, code signing, and rapid response.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Telnyx 发出警告，PyPI 上的一个 Python 包遭到入侵，发布了安全公告并给出修复步骤。这一事件凸显了 Python 生态中供应链攻击的持续风险，以及对代码签名、来源可追溯性和快速响应的需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/2vm221913gep/79q4hqxMz9w2xhBjDDA0Gl/5f1653ceb354443ad952a67afc93eadb/58db2ca1-0d1d-40c8-9ef5-a79897cb9ce9.jpeg' data-full='https://images.ctfassets.net/2vm221913gep/79q4hqxMz9w2xhBjDDA0Gl/5f1653ceb354443ad952a67afc93eadb/58db2ca1-0d1d-40c8-9ef5-a79897cb9ce9.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47545030' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47545030' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 128</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>After a measured taste of AI&#x27;s capabilities, the author decides to step away from the AI hype. The piece argues for disciplined governance, clear use-cases, and attention to long-term societal risks rather than chasing every new AI fad.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在短暂接触 AI 能力后，作者决定离开 AI 热潮。文章主张制定克制的治理框架、明确的使用场景，以及对长期社会风险的关注，而不是盲目追逐新兴的 AI 热潮。</p>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(254) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.fraunhofer.de/en/press/research-news/2026/march-2026/sealing-paper-packaging-without-adhesives.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A laser-based process that enables adhesive-free paper packaging</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一种激光工艺实现无粘合纸包装</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47558782' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47558782' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A laser-based process enables adhesive-free paper packaging, offering a sustainable alternative to traditional glues. The method promises strong seals and potential manufacturing efficiency, though it may require new equipment and process tuning. It highlights a move toward greener packaging technologies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一种基于激光的工艺实现纸包装的无粘合封口，提供比传统粘合剂更环保的替代方案。该方法可能实现强封口与潜在的制造效率提升，但需要新设备和工艺调试。体现了向更环保包装技术的转变。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(255) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.hmpcabral.com/2026/03/20/two-studies-in-compiler-optimisations/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Two studies in compiler optimisations</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>编译器优化领域的两项研究</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47478599' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47478599' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Two independent studies shed light on modern compiler optimisations, revealing how different strategies affect performance, binary size, and compile time. The findings suggest that optimization heuristics can have non-obvious trade-offs, informing developers about when and how to rely on aggressive optimizations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>两项独立研究揭示了现代编译器优化的效果，展示了不同优化策略对性能、二进制大小和编译时间的影响。研究表明优化启发式可能存在非直观的权衡，帮助开发者判断在何时、如何使用积极的优化。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Two AI agents collaborate to write and refine code, simulating a pair-programming workflow that can accelerate iteration and surface mistakes early. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍两名 AI 代理协同进行结对编程，以加速编码迭代、早期暴露问题。讨论协作策略、潜在收益，以及避免偏离与幻觉等风险所需的 guardrails。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article compares data manipulation ecosystems, highlighting Clojure&#x27;s approach with functional pipelines versus R&#x27;s data frames and Python&#x27;s pandas/polars. It discusses expressiveness, composability, and performance trade-offs when doing data wrangling at scale, plus considerations for team skill sets and ecosystem maturity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章比较了数据处理生态：Clojure 的函数式流水线方法与 R 的数据框和 Python 的 pandas/polars 的差异。讨论了表达力、组合性、以及大规模数据清洗的性能权衡，并就团队技能与生态成熟度给出思考。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(259) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.allsky7.net/#archive' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The European AllSky7 fireball network</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧洲 AllSky7 火球观测网</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47539767' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 116</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47539767' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Europe-wide AllSky7 coordinates autonomous cameras to detect and track fireballs and meteors across the night sky. The network enables large-scale data collection on asteroid and atmospheric events, contributing to research and public engagement in astronomy. Real-time alerts and archival data deepen understanding of near-Earth space.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧洲 AllSky7 全域性利用星空摄像头网络，统一协调观测火球与流星的轨迹与亮度数据。该网络扩展了天文观测覆盖范围，推动相关研究与公众参与，既有实时警报也有海量归档数据。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47482760' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 113</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47482760' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Spam is increasingly using vibe-coded signals to tailor messages to recipients&#x27; identities and contexts, aiming to bypass filters and boost engagement. The trend shows attackers exploiting cultural cues and sentiment to improve deliverability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>垃圾邮件正越来越多地使用氛围编码来针对收件人进行个性化定制，以绕过过滤器并提升投递成功率。此趋势揭示攻击者在提升可达性时利用文化线索和情感共鸣的手段。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(264) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cyber.mil/stigs/downloads' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cyber.mil serving file downloads using TLS certificate which expired 3 days ago</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cyber.mil 使用已过期的 TLS 证书提供下载</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47490816' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 112</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47490816' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 104</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An official Cyber.mil download service was served over TLS with a certificate that expired three days earlier. The lapse risks trust and introduces MITM exposure, highlighting gaps in certificate lifecycle management for critical infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cyber.mil 的下载服务在 TLS 加密中使用了三天前就到期的证书，暴露了信任与中间人攻击的风险。此事凸显关键基础设施的证书生命周期管理薄弱，需要改进。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A demonstration shows that renaming firmware packages to .zip can reveal internal files, enabling quick analysis or modification of older hardware. The clip underscores how simple packaging tricks expose security and maintenance gaps in legacy devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>演示显示将固件包改名为 .zip 即可解压出内部文件，便于对旧硬件进行快速分析或修改。视频凸显了简单的打包技巧可能暴露遗留设备的安全与维护漏洞。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece traces the birth of the world’s first electric grid, from isolated generators to a interconnected network. It explains early engineering trade-offs around generation methods, reliability, and business models that shaped the nascent electrical industry—and why those early decisions still influence grid modernization today.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文讲述世界上第一座电网的建立过程，揭示了从单一发电厂到城市级网络的技术与制度挑战。早期在发电方式、网路可靠性和商业模式等方面的权衡，至今仍影响着现代电网的改造与升级。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(274) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/03/27/apple-says-no-one-using-lockdown-mode-has-been-hacked-with-spyware/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple says no one using Lockdown Mode has been hacked with spyware</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果称使用锁定模式的用户尚未被间谍软件入侵</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47544614' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 104</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47544614' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 75</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple says there have been no verified hacks of Lockdown Mode users, reinforcing the feature as a high-security option against targeted spyware. While encouraging, it also highlights the continuous adversarial arms race and that Lockdown Mode remains a best-effort safeguard for the most exposed users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果表示正在使用锁定模式的用户尚未被间谍软件入侵，强调该功能对高风险目标具有防护效力。尽管这是一项积极信号，但也凸显攻击方的持续攻防博弈——锁定模式并非全能，且仅对最暴露的用户有显著帮助。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(275) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnbc.com/2026/03/24/arm-launches-its-own-cpu-with-meta-as-first-customer.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Arm releases first in-house chip, with Meta as debut customer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Arm 推出自研芯片，Meta 成为首任客户</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47511112' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 104</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47511112' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Arm announces its first in-house processor, with Meta as the debut customer, marking a notable shift as Arm moves toward more vertically integrated silicon. The move could influence licensing dynamics and signal growing demand for custom silicon in data-heavy workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Arm 发布首款自研处理器，Meta 成为首批客户，标志着 Arm 在向垂直整合芯片迈出重要一步。此举可能影响授权格局，并体现对面向数据密集型工作负载的自定义芯片需求上升。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A port brings the Glide driver and Glide2x support to IRIX for the Voodoo graphics setup. The project demonstrates retro-graphics nostalgia and the persistence of open-source efforts to preserve legacy hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>IRIX 系统将承载 3dfx Voodoo 的 Glide 驱动与 Glide2x 的 IRIX 端口，重现经典显卡在旧系统上的运行。此举体现了对遗留硬件的热情与开源社区的保留工作。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(277) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.chainalysis.com/blog/lessons-from-the-resolv-hack/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Resolv hack: How one compromised key printed $23M</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Resolv 黑客：单一被妥协的密钥如何打印出2300万美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47495719' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 103</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47495719' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 152</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Resolv hack shows how a single compromised signing key enabled the attacker to move 23 million dollars in crypto, underscoring the critical importance of key management and secure signing in crypto ecosystems. The post outlines breach vectors and lessons for custodial and multi-sig setups.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Resolv 黑客事件显示，一把被妥协的签名密钥让攻击者获得了2300万美元的加密资产，凸显密钥管理和托管方案的关键性。文中梳理了入侵过程与对多签/托管体系的教训。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.chainalysis.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/2026-03-attack-on-resolve-2.jpg' data-full='https://www.chainalysis.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/2026-03-attack-on-resolve-2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47498222' data-tags='Science,Health,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(278) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jamanetwork.com/journals/jama/fullarticle/2847011' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Embracing Bayesian methods in clinical trials</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在临床试验中采用贝叶斯方法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47498222' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 103</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47498222' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Advocates Bayesian methods for clinical trials as a way to use prior information and run adaptive designs, potentially speeding insights and reducing patient exposure. It also covers challenges like priors choice, computation, and regulatory acceptance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提倡在临床试验中采用贝叶斯方法，以利用先验信息并实现自适应设计，从而加速洞察并减少患者暴露；同时讨论先验选择、计算复杂性和监管接受度等挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

The lineup signals how Arm plans to balance performance, power, and software compatibility as AGI tasks scale across devices. <p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>JavaScript Is Enough argues that JS remains a remarkably capable platform for most web tasks and beyond, thanks to its ecosystem, tooling, and ubiquity. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张 JavaScript 已足以应对大多数 Web 任务，得益于丰富的生态、工具链与广泛应用范围。文章挑战对语言纯粹性的执着，提倡以务实为先的开发思路。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chronicles a personal experiment using a large language model to fill gaps in algorithm knowledge over a week, with insights on effective prompting and learning strategies. Highlights both potential and limits of AI-assisted self-education.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47561297' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(285) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://adlrocha.substack.com/p/adlrocha-what-if-ai-doesnt-need-more' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What if AI doesn&#x27;t need more RAM but better math?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如果 AI 不需要更多 RAM，而是需要更好的数学？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47561297' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 101</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47561297' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that AI efficiency could come from better math, models, and data representations rather than simply more memory. It discusses how clever mathematical reformulations and algorithmic improvements can yield higher performance with similar or even reduced RAM. The take is a reminder to focus on core abstractions and data structures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为 AI 的提升并非单纯增加内存，而是来自更好的数学、模型和数据表达。讨论通过更聪明的数学重新表达与算法改进，在 RAM 相近或减少的条件下获得更高性能。提醒我们专注于核心抽象和数据结构。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(289) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://emi-h.com/articles/a-case-against-currying.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A case against currying</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对柯里化的反对意见</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47477090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 99</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47477090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 119</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that currying can harm readability and debugging in real-world code, especially as function chains grow. The piece advocates pragmatic function design and cautions against overusing point-free styles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>指出在实际代码中，柯里化可能降低可读性和调试性，尤其当函数链日益复杂时。提倡更务实的函数设计，警惕过度使用点-free 风格。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(292) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.washingtonpost.com/travel/2026/03/28/tsa-line-sitters/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>TSA lines are so out of control that travelers are hiring line-sitters</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47563007' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 97</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47563007' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 120</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TSA wait times have grown so long that travelers are hiring line-sitters to wait in lines for them. The trend highlights the strain on security throughput and the gig-economy responses to peak travel. It also prompts discussion about efficiency, pricing, and service design in public infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>由于 TSA 排队时间过长，旅客开始雇佣排队员代为排队。这反映出安检通行能力的压力以及高峰期的临时性服务市场，同时引发对公共基础设施效率、定价与服务设计的讨论。</p>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(293) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phys.org/news/2026-03-pompeii-scars-linked-ancient-machine.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pompeii&#x27;s battle scars linked to an ancient &#x27;machine gun&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Pompeii 的战斗伤痕与古代“机枪”相关</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47453398' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 96</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47453398' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Archaeologists link Pompeii&#x27;s battle scars to an ancient rapid-fire weapon, a claim that is provocative but uncertain. If validated, it would rewrite what we know about Roman siege technology and the risks faced by the city.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>考古学家将庞贝城的战斗痕迹与古代快速发射武器相关联——尽管说法仍具争议。若相关结论成立，将改变人们对罗马战争技术的认识以及庞贝城在古代战争中的风险。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(294) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://calpaterson.com/disregard.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&quot;Disregard That&quot; Attacks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>“Disregard That” 攻击</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores a class of inputs named &#x27;Disregard That&#x27; attacks that attempt to undermine model defenses or prompt-injection safeguards. The write-up highlights how such tactics test the resilience of AI systems and suggests practical mitigations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍一种名为“Disregard That”的攻击手法，旨在绕过模型的防护或提示注入防御。文章强调这类策略对 AI 系统鲁棒性的挑战，并提出可行的缓解思路。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SCIP 的未来展望软件组件身份协议如何演进，以提升软件供应链安全、互操作性和可溯源性。文章探讨治理、采用挑战，以及这对开发者、厂商和安全团队的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(296) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/mar/27/wikipedia-bans-ai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wikipedia bans AI-generated content in its online encyclopedia</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>维基百科禁止 AI 生成的内容</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47556116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 92</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47556116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Wikipedia bans AI-generated content to preserve verifiability and human-centric editing. The move pushes contributors to rely on transparent, citable sources and may accelerate tooling to verify provenance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>维基百科宣布禁止 AI 生成内容，以维护可验证性和由人编辑的标准。此举促使编辑者坚持使用可追溯的来源，并可能推动对内容出自与出处的工具化验证。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(297) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.psychologytoday.com/us/blog/the-algorithmic-mind/202603/adults-lose-skills-to-ai-children-never-build-them' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Adults Lose Skills to AI. Children Never Build Them</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>成年人因 AI 失去技能，孩子们却从不培养这些技能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47552617' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 92</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47552617' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 84</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI-assisted workflows are eroding certain adult skills, from memory to basic calculation, while children may not develop those skills due to overreliance on automation. The piece warns about long-term cognitive and educational impacts and urges balanced use and targeted skill-building.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 辅助的工作流程正在侵蚀成年人部分技能，如记忆与基本计算，而儿童也可能因过度依赖自动化而未能培养相应能力。文章警示长期的认知与教育影响，并呼吁实现平衡使用与有针对性的技能培养。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn2.psychologytoday.com/assets/styles/manual_crop_1_91_1_1528x800/public/teaser_image/blog_entry/2026-03/vitaly-gariev-_uijcldvewc-unsplash.jpg?itok=XzXN5TLH' data-full='https://cdn2.psychologytoday.com/assets/styles/manual_crop_1_91_1_1528x800/public/teaser_image/blog_entry/2026-03/vitaly-gariev-_uijcldvewc-unsplash.jpg?itok=XzXN5TLH' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47549649' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(298) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://johnjwang.com/post/2026/03/27/why-are-executives-enabled-with-ai-but-ics-arent/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why are executives enamored with AI, but ICs aren&#x27;t?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何高管迷恋 AI，但一线工程师并不热衷？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47549649' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 92</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47549649' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 160</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Executives chase AI for strategic leverage and measurable impact, but ICs worry about rising toil, opaque ROI, and skill erosion. The note argues for aligning incentives, reducing friction in workflows, and delivering tooling that genuinely eases engineers’ workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>高管热衷于 AI，追求战略性杠杆和可衡量的影响；而一线工程师则担心工作负担增加、收益不透明和技能被边缘化。文章呼吁对齐激励、简化工作流程，并提供真正能减轻工程师负担的工具。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(300) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/DavidHavoc/ayaFlow' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AyaFlow: A high-performance, eBPF-based network traffic analyzer written in Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AyaFlow：用 Rust 编写的高性能基于 eBPF 的网络流量分析器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47563978' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 89</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47563978' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AyaFlow is a high-performance, Rust-based network traffic analyzer built on eBPF, enabling fast, safe, kernel-level visibility. It emphasizes memory safety and throughput for large-scale observability tasks. The project showcases Rust’s role in safe, efficient infrastructure tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AyaFlow 是一个用 Rust 编写、基于 eBPF 的高性能网络流量分析器，提供快速且安全的内核级可观测性。该工具强调内存安全与高吞吐，适合大规模观测任务，体现了 Rust 在基础设施工具中的应用潜力。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new JavaScript framework markets itself as SSR-first, no-build, and no-NPM, aiming at HTML-first development and simple CDN-based deployments. It challenges conventional toolchains and promises lean, fast apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个新 JavaScript 框架以 SSR 为先、无需构建和 NPM 为卖点，面向 HTML 优先的开发场景与通过 CDN 部署的简化模式。对传统工具链构成挑战，承诺提供更轻量的应用体验。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(306) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/elixir-volt/quickbeam' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: QuickBEAM – run JavaScript as supervised Erlang/OTP processes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：QuickBEAM——在监督的 Erlang/OTP 进程中运行 JavaScript</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47558094' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 86</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47558094' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>QuickBEAM enables running JavaScript inside supervised Erlang/OTP processes, bridging the ergonomics of JS with the reliability of Erlang. It demonstrates cross-language orchestration within a fault-tolerant runtime. A glimpse into practical multi-language concurrency patterns.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>QuickBEAM 使 JavaScript 可以在受监督的 Erlang/OTP 进程中运行，将 JS 的易用性与 Erlang 的可靠性结合起来，展示了在容错运行时内的跨语言协作模式。提供了一种实用的多语言并发实现思路。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(308) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jamesbaker.uk/i-quit-editing-photos/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I quit editing photos</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我不再编辑照片</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47459625' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 85</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47459625' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 126</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal essay about stepping back from manual photo editing, exploring how automation and AI tools affect creative control and time management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者分享放弃手动图片编辑的决定，探讨自动化与 AI 工具对创作控制与时间管理的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(309) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.icao.int/news/new-power-bank-restrictions-will-safeguard-international-aviation' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ICAO issued new power bank restriction on flight</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ICAO tightened restrictions on carrying power banks on flights to reduce fire risk from lithium-ion batteries, affecting passengers and airline procedures. The policy pushes manufacturers and carriers toward safer design and handling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>国际民航组织对随身携带充电宝的规定进一步收紧，限制容量并规范运输要求，以降低锂离子电池引发火灾的风险。新规将影响旅客、航空公司流程与厂商的安全设计。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(315) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/cigrainger/duckdb-hnsw-acorn' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: DuckDB community extension for prefiltered HNSW using ACORN-1</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：DuckDB 社区扩展，基于 ACORN-1 的预筛选 HNSW</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47512891' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 83</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47512891' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DuckDB gains a community extension that prefilters HNSW with ACORN-1 to speed up vector search inside analytics workloads. This keeps vectorized analytics fast without relying on external search services, benefiting ML and embedding-heavy workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DuckDB 获得一个社区扩展，将 ACORN-1 的预筛选 HNSW 引入以加速分析工作负载中的向量检索。无需外部搜索服务即可实现更快的向量分析，利于嵌入和机器学习密集型的工作流。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/cea3268c561bddc15603da5b88f8795c2b81de78005b0df0ac7614b7190ba974/cigrainger/duckdb-hnsw-acorn' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/cea3268c561bddc15603da5b88f8795c2b81de78005b0df0ac7614b7190ba974/cigrainger/duckdb-hnsw-acorn' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47504498' data-tags='Security,Open Source,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(316) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/wolfssl/wolfguard' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>WolfGuard: WireGuard with FIPS 140-3 cryptography</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>WolfGuard：带 FIPS 140‑3 加密的 WireGuard</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47504498' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 82</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47504498' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>WolfGuard is a WireGuard-focused project that implements FIPS 140-3 cryptography, aligning VPN tech with government-grade security standards. This makes it appealing for regulated industries or sensitive deployments, though it may come with certification overhead and potential compatibility considerations with standard WireGuard deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>WolfGuard 是一个聚焦于 WireGuard 的项目，实现了 FIPS 140-3 加密，确保 VPN 技术符合政府等级的安全标准。对合规行业或敏感部署具有吸引力，但可能伴随认证成本和与常规 WireGuard 部署的兼容性挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/7fdf51c47e62736c1abb29d3a75c43eed46aca4512ad25564aee9034254b9a5a/wolfSSL/wolfGuard' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/7fdf51c47e62736c1abb29d3a75c43eed46aca4512ad25564aee9034254b9a5a/wolfSSL/wolfGuard' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47467543' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(318) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/EverMind-AI/MSA' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MSA: Memory Sparse Attention</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MSA：记忆稀疏注意力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47467543' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 81</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47467543' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MSA introduces memory-sparse attention to reduce memory and compute for long-context models, enabling scalable attention with less quadratic cost. This can impact how we design and train larger sequences.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MSA 提出记忆稀疏注意力，降低长上下文模型的内存与计算需求，使注意力计算更具可扩展性，或促使更大上下文的训练与推理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/99db25ba881791a9264be8ca900db0f1a01a5d4e812d7b94bd0b225c7f5789b1/EverMind-AI/MSA' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/99db25ba881791a9264be8ca900db0f1a01a5d4e812d7b94bd0b225c7f5789b1/EverMind-AI/MSA' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47483830' data-tags='Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(319) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://americanexpress.io/migrating-the-payments-network-twice/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Migrating the American express payment network, twice</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>两次迁移美国运通支付网络</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47483830' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 81</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47483830' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post chronicles American Express&#x27;s payment network migration, detailing the challenges of moving high-availability financial rails and lessons for fintech teams migrating mission-critical services. It underscores careful API design, data migration, and risk management when changing payment networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中回顾美国运通支付网络的两次迁移，分析在迁移高可用金融系统时的挑战、架构取舍与对开发者和商家的影响。强调在支付网络迁移中需关注 API 设计、数据迁移和风控等关键要点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://americanexpress.io/_post_assets/migrating-the-payments-network-twice/img/main.jpg' data-full='https://americanexpress.io/_post_assets/migrating-the-payments-network-twice/img/main.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(320) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.waterfox.com/blog/15-years-of-forking/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>15 Years of Forking</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>十五年的分叉历史</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47545607' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 81</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47545607' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Waterfox marks 15 years as a Firefox fork, highlighting the enduring appeal and trade-offs of open-source forks. The retrospective examines how forks influence innovation, governance, and user choice, shaping a broader ecosystem beyond the original project. It also reflects on community dynamics and maintainership over time.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Waterfox 作为 Firefox 的分叉走过15年，凸显开源分叉的持续魅力与取舍。回顾探讨了分叉如何影响创新、治理与用户选择，并塑造原项目之外的生态系统，同时反映社区动态与维护者长期演变。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47469744' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(321) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.crowdsupply.com/baochip/dabao/updates/bio-the-bao-i-o-co-processor' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>BIO – The Bao I/O Co-Processor</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>BIO：Bao I/O 协处理器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47469744' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 80</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47469744' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Crowd Supply updates introduce BIO, a dedicated I/O co-processor for Bao hardware that handles GPIO and peripheral interfacing, offloading work from the main microcontroller. The design aims to boost performance, simplify firmware, and enable more deterministic timing for embedded projects, with potential power and responsiveness benefits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Crowd Supply 的更新介绍了 BIO（Bao I/O 协处理器），它作为 Bao 硬件的专用 I/O 协处理器，承担 GPIO 及外设接口的处理，减轻主控芯片的负担。此设计旨在提升性能、简化固件并实现更确定性的时序，对嵌入式项目有潜在的功耗和响应优势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.crowdsupply.com/img/1155/5148f773-7784-4467-bcb9-bddecab01155/pio-path-report_png_open-graph.jpg' data-full='https://www.crowdsupply.com/img/1155/5148f773-7784-4467-bcb9-bddecab01155/pio-path-report_png_open-graph.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(323) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nybooks.com/articles/2026/03/26/tick-tick-boom-1929-andrew-ross-sorkin/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>1929: Inside the Greatest Crash in Wall Street History</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>1929：华尔街历史上最大的崩盘内幕</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47513482' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 79</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47513482' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 65</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article dissects the 1929 crash, showing how speculation, margin debt, and information gaps precipitated a rapid collapse. It traces how the crisis spurred landmark reforms and shaped market structure, offering a reminder of how fragile financial systems remain under stress.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文剖析 1929 年崩盘的成因：投机热、杠杆与信息不对称叠加，导致市场快速崩溃。它追踪了危机如何推动里程碑式的金融改革和市场结构调整，提醒人们在压力之下金融体系的脆弱性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(324) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wheresyoured.at/the-ai-industry-is-lying-to-you/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The AI Industry Is Lying to You</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 行业在欺骗你</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47506259' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 78</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47506259' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An opinion piece argues that hype around AI overstates capabilities and obscures risks, urging skepticism and demand for transparency, governance, and responsible deployment. It questions the marketing of AGI as imminent and calls for nuance in interpreting model performance and limitations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇观点文章认为对 AI 的宣传过于夸大能力、掩盖风险，呼吁保持怀疑态度、提升透明度、加强治理，并在部署时负责任地权衡。文章质疑将通用人工智能的实现过度渲染为近在眼前，并强调对模型性能与局限性的审慎解读。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.wheresyoured.at/content/images/2024/01/wyea--1.jpeg' data-full='https://www.wheresyoured.at/content/images/2024/01/wyea--1.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A primer aimed at software engineers to demystify machine learning, clarifying capabilities, limits, and practical integration patterns. It pushes back on hype and offers actionable guidance for engineering teams adopting ML in production. The piece emphasizes pragmatic, real-world ML applications over magic.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>面向软件工程师的 ML 入门，去除神秘感，澄清能力、边界与實践落地的整合模式。文章抵制噱头，给出在生产环境中采用 ML 的可执行建议，强调务实的、真实世界的应用而非幻想。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The essay blends psychology and culture to challenge fixed age thresholds for adulthood, exploring how responsibility and identity mature across life stages. It prompts reflection on how society defines and supports coming of age.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文结合心理学与文化，质疑对成年的固定年龄门槛，探讨责任与身份如何在不同阶段成熟。引发对社会如何界定与支持成年过程的反思。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gridland lets terminal apps run in the browser, offering a seamless CLI experience across local and cloud environments. By unifying terminal interactions, it can simplify demos, remote development, and portable workflows for developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN: Gridland 让终端应用也能在浏览器中运行，打通本地 CLI 和网页界面的体验。该工具有望 Simplify 演示、远程协作和跨环境开发的工作流。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN demonstrates AI Roundtable, coordinating 200 models to debate a single question to surface diverse viewpoints and reduce single-model bias. The approach promises richer reasoning but raises concerns about cost, coordination, and result reliability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 展示了 AI 圆桌，协调 200 个模型就同一问题展开辩论，以汇聚多样观点并降低单一模型偏见。本方法在提升推理丰富度方面具有潜力，但也带来成本、协作与结果可信度等担忧。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(329) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.albany.edu/news-center/news/2026-study-security-fatigue-may-weaken-digital-defenses' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Study: &#x27;Security Fatigue&#x27; May Weaken Digital Defenses</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>研究称“安全疲劳”可能削弱数字防御</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47490150' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 77</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47490150' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study finds that security fatigue may erode digital defenses as users tire of strict practices. The findings argue for better UX, stronger defaults, and more practical security messaging.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现安全疲劳可能削弱数字防御，因为用户对严格的安全做法感到疲惫。研究建议通过更友好的用户体验、更强的默认设置和更实用的安全信息来改善。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.albany.edu/sites/default/files/styles/captioned_slider/public/2026-03/sanjay-study2-highres.jpg?itok=eD7MXzJ0' data-full='https://www.albany.edu/sites/default/files/styles/captioned_slider/public/2026-03/sanjay-study2-highres.jpg?itok=eD7MXzJ0' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(330) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.graphhopper.com/blog/2026/03/23/more-precise-elevation-data-for-graphhopper/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>More precise elevation data for GraphHopper routing engine</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>某国在思科路由器中植入后门以实施全球监视，现禁止进口外国路由器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47506279' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 74</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47506279' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A country reportedly embedded backdoors in Cisco routers to facilitate surveillance and has banned foreign-made routers. The incident highlights geopolitical risk in critical infrastructure supply chains and underscores the need for hardened, trusted hardware and diversified sourcing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有报道称某国在思科路由器中植入后门以便进行监控，现禁止进口外国路由器。这一事件凸显关键基础设施供应链的地缘政治风险，并强调对更可信任的硬件与多元采购的需求。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>After decades as a staple in US news radio, CBS News Radio is entering a new phase as its app winds down, reflecting a broader shift in how audiences consume audio news. The shift underscores the fragility of long-running radio platforms in the streaming era.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CBS 新闻广播作为长期的新闻收听渠道，即将进入新阶段，相关应用逐步下线，凸显观众获取音频新闻方式在流媒体时代的转变。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47492894' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 74</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47492894' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2022 study quantifies how serif and sans-serif typefaces fare in legibility on screens, with nuanced findings across reading tasks and sizes. The results inform typography choices for UI and accessibility, suggesting practical trade-offs between aesthetics and readability. Designers can tailor typography to context, audience, and device.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2022 年的研究比较了衬线体与无衬线体在屏幕上的可读性，结果揭示不同任务和字号下的差异。对 UI 字体选择与无障碍设计具有指导意义，强调在美观与易读之间的权衡。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(337) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sheetninja.io' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Sheet Ninja – Google Sheets as a CRUD Back End for Vibe Coders</a></p>
