<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47562288' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 74</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47562288' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 69</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sheet Ninja turns Google Sheets into a CRUD backend for Vibe Coders, enabling quick prototyping and lightweight data-backed apps without a separate database. It lowers barriers for small teams to ship functional data-driven tools. The approach invites exploration of data tooling that leverages familiar spreadsheet workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Sheet Ninja 将 Google 表格转换为 Vibe Coders 的 CRUD 后端，使团队能够在无需独立数据库的情况下快速原型化和构建数据驱动的应用。为小团队提供了更低的门槛来发布具备数据支持的工具，同时引发对利用熟悉的表格工作流来构建数据工具的思考。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(341) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/jonwiggins/optio' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Optio – Orchestrate AI coding agents in K8s to go from ticket to PR</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Optio demonstrates orchestrating AI coding agents inside Kubernetes to transform a software ticket into a pull request. It showcases scalable AI-assisted development workflows and the practical challenges of containerized automation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Optio 展示了在 Kubernetes 里编排 AI 编码代理，把一个工单推进成代码改动的 Pull Request。该项目体现了可扩展的 AI 辅助开发工作流及在容器化环境中实施自动化的实际挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(347) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/gen13-launch/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cloudflare&#x27;s Gen 13 servers: trading cache for cores for 2x performance</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cloudflare 第13代服务器：以核心换缓存 实现 2 倍性能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47495795' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 70</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47495795' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare unveils Gen 13 servers, trading cache capacity for more CPU cores. The shift yields roughly 2x performance on compute-heavy edge tasks, reflecting how edge infrastructure is prioritizing parallelism over cache for certain workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 发布 Gen 13 服务器，牺牲部分缓存以换取更多 CPU 核心。在计算密集的边缘任务上，性能提升约为 2 倍，体现边缘基础设施在某些工作负载上更强调并行性。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47513098' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 68</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47513098' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jury verdict in New Mexico finds Meta liable for failing to prevent child exploitation content, ordering $375 million in damages. The decision adds to ongoing debates about platform responsibility, safety, and regulatory risk for social networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新墨西哥州陪审团裁定 Meta 对未能阻止儿童性剥削内容负责，判罚 3.75 亿美元。此案进一步推动关于平台责任、内容安全与社交网络监管风险的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>1992 年选举中的 ANSI 艺术‘电信漫画’</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47536306' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 67</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47536306' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This retrospective revisits the ANSI art &#x27;telecomics&#x27; from the 1992 US presidential election, showing how text-mode graphics enabled political storytelling before modern web memes. It spotlights artists and online communities who used simple characters to convey satire under bandwidth limits, foreshadowing later internet culture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾 1992 年美国总统大选中的 ANSI 艺术作品“电信漫画”，展示文本模式图形如何在宽带受限时代讲述政治故事。文章聚焦创作者与网络社区如何以简单字符进行讽刺，预示后来的互联网文化演变。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(360) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jtsylve.blog/post/2026/03/18/PSpice-Encryption-Weakness' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Copy-Paste Bug That Broke PSpice AES-256 Encryption</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>拷贝粘贴错误导致 PSpice AES-256 加密失效</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47439948' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 65</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47439948' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A small copy-paste bug in PSpice&#x27;s AES-256 encryption routine breaks expected cryptographic behavior, potentially causing incorrect ciphertext or decryption failure. The write-up underscores how tiny, human errors can undermine crypto modules and stresses the need for better input handling and automated testing in crypto libraries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 PSpice 的 AES-256 加密实现中出现的一个拷贝粘贴错误打乱了原本的加密行为，可能导致密文错误或解密失败。文中强调微小的人为错误就能破坏加密模块的鲁棒性，并呼吁在加密库中加强输入处理和自动化测试。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/u/594165' data-full='https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/u/594165' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(363) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/TechEmpower/FrameworkBenchmarks/issues/10932' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sunsetting the Techempower Framework Benchmarks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>停用 Techempower 框架基准</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47497763' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47497763' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Techempower Framework Benchmarks project is being sunset, ending active maintenance and updates. The move reflects the ongoing maintenance burden and evolving ecosystems, nudging developers to rely on maintained benchmarks or run their own tests while retaining historical performance insights.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Techempower 框架基准项目将进入停止维护阶段，正式结束主动更新。此举反映出维护负担与生态变化的压力，促使开发者改为使用受维护的基准集或自行执行性能测试，同时保留历史性能快照作为参考。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(364) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sandordargo.com/blog/2026/03/25/cpp26-user-friendly-assert' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>C++26: A User-Friednly assert() macro</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>C++26：一个用户友好的 assert() 宏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47515652' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47515652' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 56</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>C++26 proposes a user-friendly assert macro to improve diagnostics and customize assertion failure handling without sacrificing performance. The change aims to reduce boilerplate and aid debugging.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>C++26 提议引入更友好的 assert 宏，以改进诊断信息并在断言失败时提供更丰富的自定义选项，同时保持性能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sandordargo.com/assets/img/SANDOR_DARGO_ROUND.JPG' data-full='https://www.sandordargo.com/assets/img/SANDOR_DARGO_ROUND.JPG' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(368) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/lightfeed/extractor' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Robust LLM Extractor for Websites in TypeScript</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个用 TypeScript 编写的网页提取工具，利用大模型从任意网站提取结构化内容，能应对混乱布局与动态页面。它为构建鲁棒的数据管线提供了更稳妥的解析能力。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(369) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.trychroma.com/research/context-1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chroma Context-1: Training a Self-Editing Search Agent</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Chroma Context-1：训练一个自我编辑的搜索代理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47534564' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47534564' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Context-1 describes training a retrieval-augmented search agent that can edit its own outputs, using feedback loops and self-refinement to improve accuracy. The approach aims to reduce hallucinations and build more trustworthy search behavior, with implications for enterprise AI tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Context-1 讲述训练一个能自我编辑输出的检索代理，通过反馈回路和自我修正来提升准确性，减少错误信息。该方法对企业级 AI 工具中的可依赖性和可信度具有启发。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47461036' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(371) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cacm.acm.org/blogcacm/tony-hoare-and-his-imprint-on-computer-science/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tony Hoare and His Imprint on Computer Science</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>托尼·霍尔对计算机科学的影响</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47461036' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47461036' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece surveys Tony Hoare&#x27;s lasting impact on CS, from formal methods like Hoare logic to concurrent programming via CSP and algorithms such as quicksort. It connects his ideas to modern software correctness, verification, and language design, illustrating how foundational theories shape practical systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章梳理了托尼·霍尔对计算机科学的深远影响，包括霍尔逻辑、并发编程的 CSP 以及快速排序等算法。将他的思想与现代软件正确性、验证和语言设计联系起来，展示基础理论如何塑造实际系统。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47546911' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(372) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.redox-os.org/news/nlnet-cap-nsmgr-cwd/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Capability-Based Security for Redox: Namespace and CWD as Capabilities</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Redox 的基于能力的安全：命名空间与当前工作目录作为能力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47546911' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47546911' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Redox OS explores capability-based security by treating namespace and current working directory as explicit capabilities. This approach aims to limit permissions to what is granted and improve isolation between processes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Redox OS 通过将命名空间与当前工作目录视为显式的能力来实现基于能力的安全，旨在把权限局限于被授予的范围，并提升进程之间的隔离性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.redox-os.org/img/logo.png' data-full='https://www.redox-os.org/img/logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(373) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://schilk.co/projects/fetsncrosses/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fets and Crosses: Tic-Tac-Toe built from 2458 discrete transistors</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>2458个离散晶体管构成的井字棋</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47511581' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47511581' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fets and Crosses documents a hardware Tic-Tac-Toe built from 2458 discrete transistors, showcasing early digital logic design and the tactile limits of hardware gaming. It&#x27;s a nostalgic tour of how brute hardware could implement simple AI-like gameplay.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《Fets and Crosses》记录了用2458个离散晶体管搭建的井字棋，展示早期数字逻辑设计以及硬件实现简单对战的局限性，带来对怀旧硬件的回顾。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47491414' data-tags='Show HN,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(374) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/jer-irl/threadprocs' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Threadprocs – executables sharing one address space (0-copy pointers)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Threadprocs — 在同一地址空间共享可执行文件（零拷贝指针）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47491414' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47491414' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Threadprocs enables multiple executables to share a single address space, enabling zero-copy data sharing and fast IPC. The approach challenges traditional process isolation and could influence future runtime and OS design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Threadprocs 允许多个可执行文件共用一个地址空间，实现零拷贝的数据共享与快速进程间通信。这一思路挑战传统进程隔离，可能影响未来的运行时与操作系统设计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/ebc22db8d4a9f5bf486d26c48714c7a4d2887b0e0c9e48e4d6612da76370b8ef/jer-irl/threadprocs' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/ebc22db8d4a9f5bf486d26c48714c7a4d2887b0e0c9e48e4d6612da76370b8ef/jer-irl/threadprocs' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(376) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://about.fb.com/news/2026/03/meta-partners-with-arm-to-develop-new-class-of-data-center-silicon/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta Partners with Arm to Develop New Class of Data Center Silicon</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 与 Arm 合作开发新一代数据中心芯片</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47506889' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 59</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47506889' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta and Arm will co-design a new class of data-center silicon optimized for AI workloads, signaling a shift toward specialized hardware in large-scale deployments. The collaboration could influence chip ecosystems and cloud hardware strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 将与 Arm 联合设计新一代面向 AI 工作负载的数据中心芯片，标志着在大规模部署中对定制化硬件的需求增加。此举可能影响芯片生态与云端硬件策略。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47518649' data-tags='Space,Science'>

<p class='hn-title'>(379) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/openyak/desktop' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenYak – An open-source Cowork that runs any model and owns your filesystem</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenYak：一个开源的协作工具，能运行任意模型并掌控你的文件系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47560380' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 59</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47560380' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenYak is an open-source coworking platform that can run any machine learning model and store data on the user&#x27;s own filesystem. Its architecture highlights data ownership and local experimentation, avoiding vendor lock-in.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenYak 是一个开源的协作平台，能够在本地运行任意模型并将数据存放在用户自有的文件系统中。此设计强调数据所有权和本地试验，避免对单一厂商的依赖。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/4361182948cc3cb7bba0663630b4aa53d5c6ce18a79192afb8f6cf102448c81c/openyak/desktop' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/4361182948cc3cb7bba0663630b4aa53d5c6ce18a79192afb8f6cf102448c81c/openyak/desktop' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47444387' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(385) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.scientificamerican.com/article/gerd-faltings-mathematician-who-proved-the-mordell-conjecture-wins-the-abel/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gerd Faltings, who proved the Mordell conjecture, wins the Abel Prize</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>格尔德·法尔汀斯因证明莫德尔猜想荣获 Abel 奖</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47444387' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47444387' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gerd Faltings solved the Mordell conjecture (Faltings&#x27; theorem), a milestone in number theory. The Abel Prize honors this landmark contribution, underscoring its lasting impact on arithmetic geometry and our understanding of rational points on curves.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>格尔德·法尔汀斯因证明莫德尔猜想而获得 Abel 奖，这是数论领域的里程碑。法尔汀斯的定理对理解有理点在高阶代数曲线上的分布具有深远影响，被公认为算术几何的重要里程碑。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.scientificamerican.com/dam/m/5688f8133cea51d2/original/GerdFaltings4_AbelPrize2026_Photo_PeterBadge_Typos1_TheAbelPrize_smaller_crop.jpg?m=1773930436.982&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://static.scientificamerican.com/dam/m/5688f8133cea51d2/original/GerdFaltings4_AbelPrize2026_Photo_PeterBadge_Typos1_TheAbelPrize_smaller_crop.jpg?m=1773930436.982&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(388) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/1st1/lat.md' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lat.md: Agent Lattice: a knowledge graph for your codebase, written in Markdown</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Lat.md：Agent Lattice——用 Markdown 编写的代码库知识图谱</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47561496' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47561496' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lat.md introduces Agent Lattice, a knowledge graph for your codebase expressed in Markdown. It links concepts, APIs, and architecture in a human-friendly format, potentially aiding cross-team understanding and documentation automation. The approach favors approachable, readable knowledge graphs over heavy tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Lat.md 推出 Agent Lattice——用 Markdown 表达的代码库知识图谱，连接概念、 API 与架构，便于团队协作与文档自动化。该方法以可读性为优先，提供一种更易被团队采纳的知识图谱形式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/319c97c446b1e1ba6ad7241d334c931920c69fc51eb57ba8444b86aca4363b93/1st1/lat.md' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/319c97c446b1e1ba6ad7241d334c931920c69fc51eb57ba8444b86aca4363b93/1st1/lat.md' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47502193' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(389) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.ydb.tech/how-io-uring-overtook-libaio-performance-across-linux-kernels-and-an-unexpected-iommu-trap-ea6126d9ef14' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>io_uring, libaio performance across Linux kernels and an unexpected IOMMU trap</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>io_uring、libaio 在不同 Linux 内核的性能对比与意外的 IOMMU 陷阱</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47502193' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47502193' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post analyzes performance of io_uring vs libaio across Linux kernel versions, noting improvements and a surprising IOMMU trap that affects certain workloads. It underscores evolving I/O APIs trade-offs and the importance of kernel version choice for latency-sensitive apps, storage stacks, and virtualization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章比较了 io_uring 与 libaio 在不同 Linux 内核版本中的性能，指出性能改进同时暴露出某些工作负载会遇到的意外 IOMMU 陷阱。强调了 I/O API 的演变及对延迟敏感应用、存储栈和虚拟化的内核版本选择的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47477525' data-tags='Programming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(390) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://carette.xyz/posts/swift_c_compatibility_with_raylib/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Testing the Swift C compatibility with Raylib (+WASM)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 Raylib 测试 Swift 与 C 的兼容性（含 WASM）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47477525' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47477525' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>It likely demonstrates building and running C/Raylib code from Swift, revealing practical tricks for cross-language game development and WASM deployment. A takeaway: cross-language bindings are feasible but require careful ABI attention.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>博文探讨了通过 Raylib 实现 Swift 与 C 的互操作性，并考虑 WebAssembly 的支持。它展示了从 Swift 构建/运行 Raylib 的 C 代码的实践技巧，凸显跨语言游戏开发和 WASM 部署的可行性，同时提醒在 ABI 级别需要留意。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47555915' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47555915' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软正预期将迎来自 2008 年以来最差的一个季度，云服务、广告收入和设备市场的压力可能共同作用，影响投资者前景与公司在 AI 与云服务上的战略布局。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(392) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.0xsid.com/blog/667mhz-machine' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The 667MHz Machine</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>667MHz 机器的回顾</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47518010' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47518010' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post revisits a machine running at 667 MHz, using it as a lens to discuss design tradeoffs in early personal computers and the limits of simplicity vs. performance. It ties historical hardware constraints to modern lessons about efficiency and reliability, offering practical takeaways for retrocomputing enthusiasts and builders of low-power systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇回顾聚焦于一台 667MHz 的机器，借此讨论早期个人电脑的设计取舍与性能极限。文章把历史硬件约束与当下的高效、可靠性需求联系起来，为复古计算爱好者和低功耗系统的构建者提供实用启示。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Matadisco proposes decentralized data discovery, enabling discovery and indexing across distributed datasets without relying on centralized catalogs. It hints at scalable, privacy-conscious data ecosystems and could impact data governance and search.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Matadisco 提出去中心化的数据发现方案，实现跨分布式数据集的发现与索引，而不依赖中心化目录。该思路有望推动可扩展且更注重隐私的数据生态与数据治理。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一架 Jazz CRJ9 在纽约某跑道上与一辆救火车相撞，致使航班时刻受到影响并启动调查以评估跑道安全与应急响应。此事故凸显地面车辆协同和航空运营中的事故处置重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(398) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/xtellect/spaces' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A single-file C allocator with explicit heaps and tuning knobs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>单文件 C 分配器：显式堆和调优参数</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47520807' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47520807' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A compact, single-file C memory allocator exposing explicit heaps and tunable knobs for fine-grained control. It serves as a practical reference for understanding allocator design, benchmarking, and embedding custom allocators without pulling in external libraries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个紧凑的单文件 C 内存分配器，提供显式堆和可调控的参数，允许对分配策略进行细粒度控制。是理解分配器设计、基准测试与在项目中嵌入自定义分配器的实用参考。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(399) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/2026/03/25/open_source_bill_opinion/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Open source isn&#x27;t a tip jar – it&#x27;s time to charge for access</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开源不是打赏箱——是时候对访问收费了</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47516189' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47516189' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that open source can&#x27;t be funded by tips alone and advocates charging for access to sustain maintenance and security; discusses viable monetization models and the trade-offs involved.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为开源项目不应只靠打赏维持，应通过对访问收费等方式实现可持续发展，文中讨论了可行的商业模型及对维护者和用户的取舍。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(402) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://newrepublic.com/article/207659/non-fiction-publishing-threat-important-ever' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cuts in publishing and book reviewing imperil the future of narrative nonfiction</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47523138' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47523138' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Publishing and review cuts threaten the vitality of narrative nonfiction, risking fewer voices, delayed discoveries, and narrower public discourse. The piece argues for sustaining literature ecosystems to foster diverse, impactful storytelling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>出版与书评的削减威胁叙事性非虚构作品的活力，可能导致声音减少、发现延迟及公共话语的狭窄。文章呼吁维持文学生态，以促进多元且有影响力的叙事。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(403) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/shanselman/status/2035110958314745891' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Scott Hanselman says he&#x27;s working on Windows local accounts</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Scott Hanselman teased work on Windows local accounts, hinting at reducing dependence on Microsoft accounts for sign-in and personalization. The exploration could enable offline or privacy-preserving user management and influence both consumer and enterprise workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Scott Hanselman 表示正在研究改进 Windows 本地账户的方案，凸显减少对微软账户依赖、提升隐私与离线使用的探索。这一方向或将带来本地化的登录与个性化管理选项，并影响企业及普通用户的使用场景。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(406) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.johndcook.com/blog/2026/03/26/table-precision/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How much precision can you squeeze out of a table?</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article dives into how much numeric precision can be preserved in tabular data, exploring data types, rounding, and representation limits. It offers practical guidance for DB design and data pipelines to avoid subtle numerical bugs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章深入探讨表格数据能保留多少数值精度，涵盖数据类型选择、舍入误差与表示范围等问题，并为数据库设计与数据处理流程提供实用建议，避免细微的数值错误。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>黑石集团首席执行官警告称，油价若升至150美元可能引发全球衰退，凸显能源冲击对增长、通胀和货币政策的宏观风险。此言论加剧了对能源价格敏感性及投资者韧性规划的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(415) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/space/2026/03/what-happens-next-with-nasas-plan-to-replace-the-iss-source-it-could-get-ugly/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>No one is happy with NASA&#x27;s new idea for private space stations</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NASA&#x27;s plan to move away from the International Space Station with private space stations faces broad discomfort over cost, governance, and safety concerns. The next steps could reshape who builds and operates orbital assets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NASA 推动以私有空间站替代国际空间站的计划引发广泛担忧，涉及成本、治理和安全等问题。未来走向可能重塑太空资产的建设和运营模式。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47484295' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47484295' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>QNX, a real-time OS, has a public release showing it running on RISC-V, underscoring portability to open hardware. The port demonstrates realtime performance and expands options for embedded developers using modern ISA.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>公开发布显示 QNX（实时操作系统）已在 RISC-V 上运行，凸显其对开源硬件的可移植性。该移植展示了实时性能，并为使用现代指令集架构的嵌入式开发者带来更多选择。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47538596' data-tags='Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(420) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sites.pitt.edu/~jdnorton/Goodies/Idealization/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Failure of the Thermodynamics of Computation (2010)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>计算热力学的失败（2010）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47538596' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47538596' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Norton’s exploration critiques the thermodynamics of computation framework, questioning energy and information cost assumptions in computation theory. It invites reconsideration of fundamental limits used to analyze information processing systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>诺顿对“计算热力学”框架提出批评，质疑在计算理论中对能量与信息成本的假设。该讨论促使人们重新审视分析信息处理系统时使用的基本极限。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://sites.pitt.edu/~jdnorton/Goodies/Idealization/A_block.gif' data-full='https://sites.pitt.edu/~jdnorton/Goodies/Idealization/A_block.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(421) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.edwardfeser.com/unpublishedpapers/microphysics.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Epistemology of Microphysics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微物理学的认识论</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47517306' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47517306' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Edward Feser’s exploration of microphysics epistemology examines how we know microphysical realities through experiments and models, touching on realism and theory-ladenness. The piece invites readers to reflect on how foundational assumptions shape scientific inference in physics and computation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>爱德华·费塞尔对微物理学认识论的探讨，考察我们如何通过实验与模型认识微观现实，涉及真实论与理论负载性等议题。该文促使读者反思在物理与计算等领域中，基础假设如何影响科学推断。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>As airports struggle with delays, rail travel offers a different lens on U.S. mobility from Georgia. The piece contrasts air infrastructure fragility with a slower, people-centered rail narrative.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在机场延误普遍时，佐治亚州的深夜铁路提供了美国出行 otra 的不同视角。文中对比了航空系统的脆弱性与铁路的缓慢、以人为本的出行叙事。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(424) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ohmjs.org/blog/2026/03/12/peg-to-wasm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ohm&#x27;s Peg-to-WASM Compiler</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ohm 的 Peg→WASM 编译器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47539165' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47539165' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ohm&#x27;s Peg-to-WASM compiler translates PEG grammars into WebAssembly, enabling fast, in-browser parsers without host language overhead. It signals progress in bringing language tooling to the edge.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ohm 的 Peg-to-WASM 编译器将 PEG 语法转译为 WebAssembly，使浏览器端解析器实现更高性能，降低宿主语言开销，推动边缘计算的语言工具发展。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47500337' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(427) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://terrytao.wordpress.com/2026/03/23/local-bernstein-theory-and-lower-bounds-for-lebesgue-constants/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Local Bernstein theory, and lower bounds for Lebesgue constants</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>局部伯恩斯坦理论与 Lebesgue 常数下界</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47500337' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47500337' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tao surveys local Bernstein theory and derives lower bounds for Lebesgue constants, tying into interpolation stability and convergence rates. The discussion sheds light on how local structures influence global approximation error. The takeaway is a deeper intuition for why certain interpolation schemes behave poorly as degree grows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>泰勒探讨了局部伯恩斯坦理论，并给出 Lebesgue 常数的下界，与插值稳定性和收敛速率相关。讨论揭示了局部结构如何影响全局近似误差，帮助理解某些插值方案在次数增大时为何表现不佳。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://terrytao.wordpress.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/cheby.png' data-full='https://terrytao.wordpress.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/cheby.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Zero Days argues electric motorcycles pose security risks in software, connectivity, and OTA updates. It emphasizes the need for secure software supply chains and robust security practices in connected vehicles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出电动摩托车在软件、连接性和 OTA 更新方面存在安全隐患，强调在互联汽车领域建立安全的软件供应链和严格的安全实践。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(430) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gekkio.fi/blog/2021/power-consumption-of-game-boy-flash-cartridges/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Power consumption of Game Boy flash cartridges (2021)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Game Boy 闪存卡耗电量（2021）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47498329' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47498329' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Measured power draw of Game Boy flash cartridges across use cases shows notable variation between models, affecting handheld battery life. The findings help retro-gaming enthusiasts choose energy-efficient peripherals and inform designers of tradeoffs between speed, compatibility, and power use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对多款 Game Boy 闪存卡在运行与待机等场景的耗电量进行了对比测量，结果显示不同设计存在显著功耗差异。对于追求续航的复古玩家以及设计者来说，这些数据有助于在性能、兼容性和能效之间做出权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47557392' data-tags='Open Source,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(431) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sylve.io/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sylve – Proxmox Alternative by FreeBSD</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Sylve：基于 FreeBSD 的 Proxmox 替代方案</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47557392' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 40</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47557392' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sylve is a FreeBSD-based virtualization platform offering Proxmox-like management and features, potentially with bhyve-based virtualization, ZFS support, and containers. It presents an alternative for users seeking a BSD-centric stack.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Sylve 是一个基于 FreeBSD 的虚拟化平台，提供类似 Proxmox 的管理与功能，可能使用 bhyve、ZFS 等技术，面向偏好 BSD 堆栈的用户。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://sylve.io/og-default.png' data-full='https://sylve.io/og-default.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(432) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/alefore/mini_svg/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Minimalist library to generate SVG views of scientific data</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：用于生成科学数据 SVG 视图的极简库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47492871' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 40</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47492871' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introduces a tiny library that renders scientific data as SVG visuals, prioritizing simplicity and reproducibility over feature creep. It suits quick plots for docs, dashboards, or educational demos, with easy integration into static pages.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍一个极简库，用于把科学数据渲染成 SVG 图表，强调简单、可重复的可视化。适合快速文档、仪表盘或教学演示的静态页面集成。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(434) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nbcnews.com/tech/tech-news/openai-shuttering-sora-video-generating-service-rcna264989' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI shutting down Sora app</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 将关闭 Sora 视频生成应用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47509036' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47509036' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI is shutting down Sora, its video-generation app, as part of a broader shift away from consumer-facing tools. The move illustrates how AI companies prune products to focus on core platforms and APIs that support a broader developer ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 将关闭 Sora 视频生成应用，体现出对产品组合的聚焦与资源再分配的策略取向。此举对依赖 Sora 构建应用的开发者意味着调整和替代方案的需要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media-cldnry.s-nbcnews.com/image/upload/t_nbcnews-fp-1200-630,f_auto,q_auto:best/rockcms/2025-10/251006-Sora-2-openai-aa-237-1e9e42.jpg' data-full='https://media-cldnry.s-nbcnews.com/image/upload/t_nbcnews-fp-1200-630,f_auto,q_auto:best/rockcms/2025-10/251006-Sora-2-openai-aa-237-1e9e42.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47455961' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(435) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://martinfowler.com/articles/distributed-objects-microservices.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microservices and the First Law of Distributed Objects (2014)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微服务与分布式对象的第一定律（2014）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47455961' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47455961' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Martin Fowler revisits the relationship between microservices and distributed objects, stressing explicit boundaries and asynchronous communication over naive remote method calls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Martin Fowler 将分布式对象的思想与微服务联系起来，强调应以明确边界和异步通信来避免远程方法调用的陷阱。文章指出延迟、部分失败等问题，以及在规模化系统中维持清晰契约的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://martinfowler.com/articles/images/distributed-objects-microservices/local-remote.png' data-full='https://martinfowler.com/articles/images/distributed-objects-microservices/local-remote.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(436) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://datahike.io/notes/collaborate-without-infrastructure/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Memory That Collaborates</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>协作记忆</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47535863' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47535863' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece introduces the concept of memory architectures designed to coordinate across services with minimal infrastructure, enabling collaborative workflows. It argues for lightweight, provenance-friendly memory management that scales in distributed systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章提出一种“协作记忆”的概念，强调在跨服务协作中以最小化基础设施的方式管理内存，以支持分布式系统中的轻量化协同工作与可溯源性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://datahike.io/brand/datahike-logo.svg' data-full='https://datahike.io/brand/datahike-logo.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(437) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/intel/device-modeling-language' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Intel Device Modeling Language for virtual platforms</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英特尔用于虚拟平台的设备建模语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47465197' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47465197' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Intel&#x27;s Device Modeling Language targets virtual platforms, enabling precise hardware behavior modeling for faster simulations and co-design. It accelerates virtual prototyping of chips and software-hardware integration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英特尔的设备建模语言面向虚拟平台，旨在实现更精准的硬件行为建模，从而加速仿真、原型设计和软硬件协同开发。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(438) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cursor.com/blog/fast-regex-search' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fast regex search: indexing text for agent tools</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>快速正则搜索：为代理工具对文本建立索引</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47499242' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47499242' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post outlines fast regex search by indexing text for agent tools, describing pre-processing, inverted indexes, and caching techniques. It includes practical benchmarks and guidance for building responsive text-enabled assistants.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中介绍了为代理工具进行文本快速正则搜索的索引化方案，涵盖预处理、倒排索引与缓存等技术，并给出基准和实用建议，帮助构建更快的文本驱动助手。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(441) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://discourse.ubuntu.com/t/streamlining-secure-boot-for-26-10/79069' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ubuntu wants to strip some of GRUB features in 26.10 for security purposes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ubuntu 将在 26.10 版本中精简 GRUB 部分功能以提升安全性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47517885' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47517885' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ubuntu plans to streamline GRUB features in 26.10 to bolster security and simplify Secure Boot, reducing attack surfaces and maintenance friction for users and vendors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ubuntu 将在 26.10 版本中精简 GRUB 功能，以提升安全性并简化 Secure Boot 的实现，降低攻击面并减轻用户和厂商的维护负担。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues for clearer, explicit control over the Go toolchain version in projects, criticizing automation that picks a version without user intent. It advocates reproducible builds and better module/version hygiene for developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章呼吁在项目中对 Go 工具链版本保持更明确的控制，批评会自动选择版本的做法。倡导可复现的构建与更好的模块/版本治理。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes achieving 25 Gbps over standard 10 GbE Direct Attach Copper by optimizing PHY, cabling, and perhaps signaling techniques. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍在标准的 10 GbE 直连铜缆上通过优化物理层、线缆和编码等手段实现 25 Gbps 的传输速率。此举有望提升数据中心的带宽密度和单位成本效益，但需要权衡铜缆的物理极限和现有接口的向后兼容性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(453) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.metropolitanreview.org/p/the-last-contract' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Last Contract: William T. Vollmann&#x27;s Battle to Publish an Epic (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《最后的合约》：威廉·沃尔曼出版史诗的斗争</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47526473' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 33</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47526473' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Last Contract profiles William T. Vollmann&#x27;s strenuous effort to publish an epic, examining the endurance, negotiations, and friction within literary publishing. It offers a window into the challenges long-form fiction faces in the modern era. The piece blends literary critique with publishing industry observer insights.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《最后的合约》描绘威廉·沃尔曼为出版史诗所经历的艰苦斗争，探讨写作耐心、谈判与出版环节的摩擦。文章揭示了当代长篇小说在现代出版环境中所面临的挑战，并结合文学批评与出版行业观察提供洞见。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(456) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/pc-components/sound-cards/comparison-of-usd4-000-boutique-audio-cable-to-usd7-amazon-basics-cable-shows-audiophiles-waste-a-lot-of-money-scientific-audio-equipment-analysis-with-analyzer-shows-no-difference-in-quality' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Scientific audio equipment analysis with analyzer shows no difference in quality</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>使用分析仪对科学音频设备分析，显示质量无显著差异</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47564356' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 31</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47564356' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analyzer-based comparison of audio gear shows no meaningful difference in sound quality between boutique and basic cables. The result challenges common audiophile assumptions and advocates for rigorous, reproducible testing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过分析仪对音频设备的对比显示，精品线与普通线之间在音质上无显著差异。这一结果挑战了常见的发烧友假设，提倡进行更为严格、可重复的测试。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/VHGxfUxJPGwKY85Y3Hj79c-1920-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/VHGxfUxJPGwKY85Y3Hj79c-1920-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(458) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17099' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Vectorization of Verilog Designs and its Effects on Verification and Synthesis</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47442194' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47442194' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ArXiv paper explores how vectorizing Verilog designs affects verification and synthesis, showing how bit-vector representations can impact equivalence checking and hardware synthesis results. The findings matter for hardware designers seeking better verification performance and predictable synthesis outcomes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>论文探讨 Verilog 设计向量化对验证和综合的影响，提示位向量表示可能改变等价性检查与硬件综合结果。对硬件设计者而言，这些发现有助于提升验证性能和预测综合行为。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN presents a dataset that catalogs public transit systems—lines, stations, railcars, and historical context—into an accessible data resource. This enables researchers and developers to build analytics, visualizations, and tools that illuminate urban mobility. The project lowers barriers for transit data experimentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 提供了一个聚合公共交通系统数据的数据集，涵盖线路、站点、车厢及历史背景，成为可访问的研究与开发资源。该数据使研究人员和开发者能够开展分析、可视化与工具开发，降低了进入城市交通数据分析的门槛。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(462) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ft.com/content/805f78f3-8da3-4fc0-b860-207a859ac723' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Will the AI data centre boom become a $9T bust?</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47559948' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47559948' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The FT piece weighs whether the AI data center investment boom will overbuild, exposing risks from energy costs, power consumption, and silicon supply constraints. It frames the debate around long-term demand, efficiency gains, and potential market correction for hyperscale infra.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FT 分析称 AI 数据中心投资热潮是否会过度扩张，暴露能源成本、用电量和芯片供给约束等风险。讨论长期需求、效率提升与超大规模基础设施的潜在回调。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(463) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bloomberg.com/graphics/2026-iran-war-hormuz-closure-oil-shock' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Strait of Hormuz Oil Shock Is Now Heading West</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Oil-market disruption tied to the Hormuz Strait is spilling over to Western markets, raising price volatility and supply concerns. The geopolitical risk translates into broader energy tech and policy implications for global supply chains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>因霍尔木兹海峡引发的油市冲击正在波及西方市场，推高价格波动与供应担忧。这一地缘政治风险对全球供应链的能源科技与政策层面产生广泛影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47508078' data-tags='Career'>

<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(465) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://newsletter.thelongcommit.com/p/the-case-for-becoming-a-manager' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The case for becoming a manager</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>成为管理者的理由</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47508078' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47508078' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that moving into management can amplify your impact beyond individual contributions, shaping product strategy, hiring, and culture. It discusses the trade-offs, such as reduced hands-on coding time, and offers guidance on when and how to prepare for a transition. For engineers who want broader influence, management can be a deliberate, high-leverage career path.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文认为走向管理岗位可以把个人影响力放大到产品方向、团队培养和文化建设等领域，超越单打独斗的贡献。文中权衡了对编码实践的减少等取舍，并给出何时及如何为这一转型做准备的建议。对寻求更大影响力的工程师来说，管理是一个有意且高杠杆的职业路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!aNz7!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F05c70c7d-e58f-49e4-9aba-a26338d1dc1c_3440x1920.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!aNz7!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F05c70c7d-e58f-49e4-9aba-a26338d1dc1c_3440x1920.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47538433' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 27</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47538433' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>HandyMKV scripts automate ripping with MakeMKV and transcoding with HandBrake, enabling repeatable, batch-ready media workflows. It highlights command-line automation and options for metadata handling, error recovery, and scaling up personal libraries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>HandyMKV：面向 MakeMKV 与 HandBrake 自动化的实用工具，支持批量处理与可重复工作流。强调命令行自动化、元数据处理与大规模图库管理。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Monado, an open-source XR runtime, has become foundational for OpenXR runtimes. The result is more cohesive cross-platform experiences for developers and users.</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>追踪我的睡眠周期的黑客</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47532212' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47532212' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hackers tracked the author&#x27;s sleep cycle, illustrating how attackers can exploit IoT sleep trackers and wearables to infer sensitive personal routines. It serves as a cautionary tale for personal data hygiene in connected ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>黑客追踪了作者的睡眠周期，揭示攻击者如何利用物联网睡眠追踪设备推断个人作息。此文凸显了消费健康设备的隐私与安全风险，以及对设备级保护的需求，提醒人们在连接生态中关注个人数据安全。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>惊呆了。谷歌把光纤业务卖给私募股权</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Om Malik notes Google&#x27;s surprising move to flip its fiber assets to private equity, signaling a pivot in network infrastructure strategy. The deal could affect fiber deployment speed, service quality, and competitive dynamics in broadband.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Om Malik 指出谷歌将光纤资产转至私募股权的消息，标志着其网络基础设施策略的转变。这笔交易或影响光纤部署速度、服务质量及宽带竞争格局。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(472) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://victoriaritvo.com/blog/robust-semantle-solver/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Solving Semantle with the Wrong Embeddings</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用错误嵌入破解 Semantle 的方法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47507576' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 24</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47507576' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A deep dive into solving Semantle with intentionally misaligned embeddings, showing that robustness can come from exploiting the structure of the search space even when representations are imperfect. It exposes both strengths and potential weaknesses of embedding-based puzzle solving.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>深入探讨使用故意错位的嵌入来解决 Semantle，展示即使嵌入表示不完美，仍可通过抓住搜索空间结构来实现解题。揭示了基于嵌入的推断在鲁棒性方面的优点与潜在弱点。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(475) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://yoinkify.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Yoink – Spotify to lossless with full metadata, self-hostable, ad-free</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Yoink——自托管的无损音乐方案，完整元数据、无广告</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47521910' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 22</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47521910' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Yoink appears to be a self-hosted pipeline turning Spotify music into lossless files with complete metadata, enabling ad-free playback locally. It highlights community-driven, privacy-conscious music workflows and the feasibility of local music ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Yoink 似乎是一个自托管的解决方案，将 Spotify 音乐转换为无损文件并保留完整元数据，提供本地离线无广告播放。此举体现了社区驱动的隐私友好型音乐工作流，以及本地化音乐生态系统的可行性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(476) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://scitechdaily.com/scientists-just-broke-the-solar-power-limit-everyone-thought-was-absolute/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Scientists Just Broke the Solar Power Limit Everyone Thought Was Absolute</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科学家打破了被普遍认为是绝对的太阳能功率极限</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47525093' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 20</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47525093' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers report surpassing a long-debated solar power efficiency limit, hinting at advances in materials or cell architecture. The claim, if verified, could accelerate improvements in photovoltaic technology and renewable energy deployment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员声称突破长期受到关注的太阳能效率极限，可能涉及新材料或电池结构的进步。如经证实，或将推动光伏技术和可再生能源部署的加速。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在终端中渲染复杂脚本与 OSC 66</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47475826' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 20</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47475826' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Discusses approaches to rendering complex scripts (likely multilingual or Indic scripts) in terminal environments and related OSC 66 control sequences. It covers fonts, shaping, and terminal capabilities critical for robust multilingual support.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨在终端环境中渲染复杂脚本（可能是多语言或印刷字形）以及相关的 OSC 66 控制序列。涵盖字体、字形排版与终端能力等关键点，以实现对多语言的稳健支持。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(478) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.usatoday.com/story/news/nation/2026/03/25/tsa-line-airport-wait-times-highest-in-history/89314232007/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Airport wait times are longest in TSA history, agency says</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TSA 史上最长的机场安检等待时间</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47521949' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 19</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47521949' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TSA reports record-long airport security lines as travel rebounds, highlighting staffing, process efficiency, and queue management. The delay underscores challenges in scaling passenger throughput and prompts attention to technology-assisted screening and process optimization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TSA 报告机场安检排队时间创历史新高，显示旅行需求回暖与安检能力之间的矛盾，同时推动对流程与技术的改进关注。</p>
